<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据合成方法依赖启发式提示词控制，属于黑盒范式，无法理解单个样本与模型学习动力学之间的交互关系，缺乏对数据质量的精细可控性。

Method: 提出一个电路接地框架，沿可学习性、挑战性、对齐性三个效用轴量化数据质量；利用机制可解释性发现并因果验证控制这些效用信号的模型内部电路；将这些电路作为可控接口替代启发式提示词进行数据生成；进一步提出SAMS（阶段感知机械调度）根据模型优化的动态需求调度电路引导的数据。

Result: 在多选题问答任务上，该方法生成了比提示词基线更多样化且精确控制的数据，持续提升了下游性能与校准效果。

Conclusion: 本文确立了数据生成的白盒范式，将机制可解释性从纯粹的分析工具拓展为实用、可控的生成接口，为可解释数据生成提供了原则性框架。

Abstract: While recent advances in data synthesis aim to curate high-quality datasets, most generation pipelines still rely on heuristic prompt-based control. This black-box paradigm provides limited insight into how individual samples interact with a model's underlying learning dynamics. To bridge this gap, we propose a circuit-grounded framework that connects training-dynamics-based data valuation with mechanistic interpretability (MI). Specifically, we conceptualize data quality along three complementary utility axes, learnability, challenge, and alignment. First, we uncover specialized model-internal circuits that causally govern these utility signals. Then, moving beyond heuristic prompting toward mechanistic control, we leverage these circuits as controllable interfaces, actively steering generation to produce utility-targeted data. Building on this capability, we introduce SAMS (Stage-Aware Mechanistic Scheduling), which schedules circuit-steered data according to the model's evolving optimization needs. Experiments on multiple-choice QA tasks demonstrate that our approach yields precisely controlled data with greater diversity than prompt-based baselines, consistently improving downstream performance and calibration. Ultimately, this work establishes a principled white-box paradigm for interpretable data generation, pioneering the use of MI not just as an analytical tool, but as a practical, controllable interface.

</details>


### [37] [Knowing When to Ask for Help: Bayesian Self-Escalation in Hierarchical LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24087)
*Nadeem Shaikh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体系统中的"代内委派"（intra-generation delegation）机制：智能体在自身推理过程中实时识别出自己可能失败，并将控制权转移给更强的模型。作者将此建模为贝叶斯最优停止问题，给出了闭式解、最优策略的时序阈值性质、指数分离与遗憾界等理论保证，并通过仿真和真实模型验证（Qwen2.5-Coder 1.5B→7B）证明了该方法优于事后路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM委派系统要么在推理前（路由器选模型）决定委派，要么在推理完成后（验证器评分后可能重试）再委派，缺少"推理过程中动态识别失败风险并即时转移"的第三种机制。作者希望引入这种代内委派机制，通过在线学习的能力后验估计来尽早判断任务是否会失败，从而避免无效推理开销。

Method: 1) 将代内委派建模为贝叶斯最优停止问题，其中"能力后验"（competence posterior）是对智能体最终任务成功概率的在线估计，其充分统计量从带标签的轨迹中学习（而非直接从原始熵读取）。2) 推导出贪心（myopic）升级阈值的闭式解。3) 通过动态规划刻画最优策略，并证明最优策略是时变阈值，且对原始信号无需任何形状假设。4) 证明在Chernoff信息率下预言信念（oracle belief）的指数分离，以及由后验校准控制的遗憾界。5) 给出有限样本保证：用n条带标签校准轨迹时，plug-in策略的遗憾以1/sqrt(n)速率衰减。

Result: 受控仿真实验验证了全部理论预测（包括1/sqrt(n)速率）。在真实模型验证中（Qwen2.5-Coder 1.5B→7B代码级联，MBPP数据集257个任务），确认了三条预注册预测中的两条：升级边界（escalation frontier）在同等成本下优于事后路由；累积能力信念的区分度随生成过程上升。

Conclusion: 代内委派提供了一种比事后路由更具优势的委派范式，理论基础完善（闭式解、时变阈值最优性、指数分离、有限样本保障），并有仿真与真实模型验证支撑，为LLM智能体的高效资源调配提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Current LLM agent systems decide delegation before reasoning begins (a router picks a model) or after a response is complete (a verifier scores it and may retry). We study a third regime: an agent that recognises, during its own reasoning, that it is unlikely to succeed and transfers control to a stronger model. We formulate intra-generation delegation as a Bayesian optimal-stopping problem over a learned competence posterior -- an online estimate of the agent's eventual task success whose sufficient statistics are learned from labelled trajectories, not read off raw entropy. We derive the myopic escalation threshold in closed form, characterise the optimal policy via dynamic programming, and prove that the optimal policy is a time-varying threshold with no shape assumption on the raw signal. We further prove exponential separation of the oracle belief at the Chernoff-information rate of the signal, a regret bound governed by the calibration of the posterior, and a finite-sample guarantee: with n labelled calibration trajectories the deployed plug-in policy's regret decays as 1/sqrt(n). A controlled simulation study confirms each prediction of the theory, including the predicted 1/sqrt(n) rate. We additionally report a real-model validation on a Qwen2.5-Coder 1.5B->7B code cascade (MBPP, 257 tasks), confirming two of three pre-registered predictions: the escalation frontier dominates post-hoc routing at equal cost, and the cumulative competence belief's discrimination rises over generation.

</details>


### [38] [The Sharp Tail of Uniform Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24098)
*Pahan Dewasurendra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构造了第一个同时满足高概率和矩意义上最优界的γ-一致稳定学习问题，证明了均匀稳定性的泛化差距在高置信度下对log(1/δ)的线性依赖是可以由实际有界损失算法实现的，从而填补了理论空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知的γ-一致稳定算法存在O(γ log(1/δ) + L√(log(1/δ)/n))的对数自由上界，但其线性依赖log(1/δ)项是否能被实际的有界损失学习算法实现仍然是开放问题。已有的构造仅适用于点状值域随n增长的辅助弱相关随机变量，而已知的学习下界仅在常数概率下成立。

Method: 对于任意n、稳定性水平γ和损失界L，构造一个确定性的γ-一致稳定学习问题，其尾部满足同时对所有1≤p≤cn成立的概率下界。构造采用普通的有界绝对值损失回归问题（常数标签），核心是多重尺度的稀有Rademacher特征集合，通过坐标方向上的ramp函数实现上确界范数稳定性，利用奇对称化最大值将唯一的极值特征转化为γp阶的间隙而不违反损失界，几何间隔的ramp将所有置信水平融入同一问题。

Result: 构造的学习问题同时满足所有p的尾部下界P(R(A_S)-R_S(A_S) ≥ c'min{L, γp+L√(p/n)}) ≥ e^{-p}，与对数自由上界结合，确定了均匀稳定性在高概率和矩意义上最优依赖关系，精确到通用常数。

Conclusion: 该工作完全解决了均匀稳定性泛化界是否紧的问题：线性log(1/δ)依赖是可实现的，且上界和下界匹配到通用常数，从而得到均匀稳定性泛化差距的精确最优刻画。

Abstract: Uniform stability controls how much one training example can change the loss at any test point. A new logarithmic-free upper bound shows that a $γ$-uniformly stable algorithm with loss in $[0,L]$ has generalization gap at most $O \left(γ\log(1/δ) +L\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/δ)}{n}}\right)$ with probability $1-δ$. Whether an actual bounded-loss learning algorithm can realize the linear dependence on $\log(1/δ)$ has remained open. The known construction realizes it only for auxiliary weakly dependent random variables whose pointwise range grows with $n$. The known learning lower bound holds only at constant probability. We close this gap. For every $n$, stability level $γ$, and loss bound $L$, we construct one deterministic $γ$-uniformly stable learning problem whose tail satisfies, simultaneously for $1\le p\le c n$, $\mathbb P \left( R(A_S)-R_S(A_S) \ge c'\min \left\{L,γp+L\sqrt{p/n}\right\} \right)\ge e^{-p}.$ The construction is ordinary bounded absolute-loss regression with constant labels. Its key is a multiscale collection of rare Rademacher features. A coordinatewise ramp is stable in sup norm, while an odd symmetrized maximum converts a unique extreme feature into a gap of order $γp$ without violating the loss bound. Geometrically spaced ramps put all confidence levels into the same problem. Together with the logarithmic-free upper bound, this determines the optimal high-probability and moment dependence of uniform stability up to universal constants.

</details>


### [39] [Structured Frequency-Domain Evidence for LLM-Based Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24113)
*Jungwook Seo,Sangwon Son,Minjeong Kim,Seungmin Han,Seojin Yoo,Sungyong Baik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种证据增强的零样本LLM时间序列异常检测框架，通过FFT提取全局和局部频域证据，与索引去季节化时域证据结合，在多个多模态大模型上验证了频域证据对TSAD性能的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的时间序列异常检测方法主要依赖时域证据（如索引值、图像或去季节化表示），忽略了频谱结构的隐含信息。然而时间序列异常不仅表现为点状偏差，还可能体现为周期性结构的变化（如周期位移或局部振荡波动），需要显式的频域证据来增强检测能力。

Method: 提出一个证据增强的零样本TSAD框架：保留索引化的去季节化时域观测，同时利用FFT计算紧凑的频域证据。频域证据在两个分辨率上构建：全局频域证据总结序列级周期性上下文，局部频域证据捕获时间局部化的频谱偏离。

Result: 在AnomLLM上使用InternVL2-LLaMA3-76B、Qwen2.5-VL-72B-Instruct、Gemini-2.5-Flash和GPT-4o等多个多模态LLM，并在TSB-AD-U子集上评估，结果显示显式的频域证据显著提升了基于LLM的TSAD基线性能。

Conclusion: 频域证据可以作为索引化和去季节化时域输入的互补信息，有效增强零样本LLM时间序列异常检测能力。

Abstract: Time-series anomalies can appear not only as pointwise deviations but also as changes in recurring temporal structure, such as shifted periodicity or localized oscillatory fluctuations. However, existing LLM-based time-series anomaly detection methods mainly expose time-domain evidence through indexed values, plots, or de-seasonalized representations, leaving spectral structure implicit. We propose an evidence-augmented zero-shot TSAD framework that preserves indexed de-seasonalized observations while adding compact frequency-domain evidence computed with the Fast Fourier Transform (FFT). The evidence is constructed at two resolutions: global frequency-domain evidence summarizes sequence-level periodic context, while local frequency-domain evidence captures time-localized spectral departures. Experiments on AnomLLM with InternVL2-LLaMA3-76B, Qwen2.5-VL-72B-Instruct, Gemini-2.5-Flash, and GPT-4o, together with evaluation on the TSB-AD-U subset, show that explicit frequency-domain evidence improves LLM-based TSAD baselines. Abstract: Phase-field modeling of brittle fracture removes the need to track cracks explicitly by recasting their evolution as the minimization of an energy functional. In return it requires a discretization dense enough to resolve a localization band whose width is set by a regularization length and whose path is not known in advance. We propose a mesh-free discretization in which a single neural network represents the displacement and phase fields and is trained by minimizing the incremental energy directly. The coordinates enter the network through a multiresolution feature encoding built from $C^1$ quadratic B-spline grids, so the finest scale the representation can express is set by choice rather than reached through slow training, and the energy is estimated by stratified Monte Carlo integration on points redrawn at every optimizer iteration. This pairing proves critical, since the crack fails to advance both when the integration points are held fixed and when the encoding is too coarse to represent the band, while each ingredient tolerates a wide range of settings once the other is in place. Because the representation is globally $C^1$, the second- and the fourth-order fracture energy densities run on the identical discretization. Across six problems, from single-edge-notched tension and shear to a thick-walled ring on a single spline patch, the computed load-displacement curves follow staggered finite element references at matched regularization length, with peak loads within about 1% on the single-edge-notched tests and within 8% where the crack pattern changes topology. On a public benchmark dataset of random multi-crack configurations the method classifies the active or dormant state of 90% of the seeded cracks in twenty zero-shot runs, where the deep Ritz baseline of the dataset authors fails.

Method: 核心方法包括：(1) 将推荐重构为成对二分类预测，联合学习用户特征和商品特征；(2) 通过负采样和噪声注入增强学习表示；(3) 对长对话上下文使用注意力池化处理对话片段，并对比TF-IDF和句嵌入基线；(4) 探索多种架构（双塔模型、DeepFM及其变体）和损失函数（如对比损失），以CatBoost作为对照基线进行评估。

Conclusion: 从树模型迁移到成对二分类深度推荐模型是可行且有效的，尤其适用于需要动态整合对话上下文和多模态特征的生产级对话推荐系统。注意力池化处理长对话上下文、负采样和噪声注入增强表示等关键技术为此类迁移提供了可复制的经验。

Abstract: Product catalogs in fast-moving service businesses are shifting from static, independently priced SKUs toward dynamically bundled, discount-coupled offerings--a shift that strains the tree-based classifiers traditionally preferred for sparse and highly imbalanced data. These classifiers assume a fixed, slowly changing label space and struggle to incorporate multimodal signals such as tabular data and transcripts. We present the migration of a live, production conversational recommendation system from a gradient-boosted multiclass model to a pairwise-binary deep recommender. Because this system is critical to ecosystem growth initiatives and downstream features like dynamic pitching--surfacing the most relevant pitch text to a support agent in real time during a live customer conversation--maintaining live recommendation quality was a non-negotiable constraint. We detail the techniques that made this migration successful--reformulating recommendation as pairwise binary prediction to learn jointly from user and item features, and enhancing learned representations via negative sampling and noise injection. To efficiently incorporate long, live conversation context, we apply attention pooling over transcript chunks and benchmark it against TF-IDF and sentence-embedding baselines. Finally, we explore multiple architectures (including two-tower models, DeepFM, and their variants) and loss functions such as contrastive loss. Evaluating against a CatBoost baseline across all conversational stages, we demonstrate that our approach achieves parity at conversation beginning and outperforms at later conversational stages.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: RoFB是一种测试时干预方法：将循环模型的中间预测概率转换为token-wise成对耦合力，注入到潜在动态（latent dynamics）中，从而在不重训练的情况下闭环引导模型的推理方向。这是一种"闭环"控制机制，区别于传统"开环"地增加步数或采样轨迹。

Result: 在三种循环模型（AKOrN、ItrSA++、TRM）上、Sudoku和Maze两个任务共六个模型-任务组合中，RoFB在四个组合上取得明显提升。其性能超越了单纯增加推理步数或从多条轨迹中筛选所能达到的效果，同时计算成本相当或更低。

Conclusion: 闭环引导潜在动态可以作为循环推理模型的一种补充性推理时控制机制，表明利用模型自身读出信息来干预潜在状态动力学是一条有效的、无需重训练的性能提升路径。

Abstract: Recurrent models, which repeatedly update latent states with shared computation blocks, have emerged as powerful architectures for solving complex reasoning tasks. Existing inference-time methods scale computation by running more steps or sampling more trajectories, but ignore information revealed within each trajectory. Here we show that recurrent models can be improved at inference time by using their own readout probabilities to steer latent dynamics without retraining. We introduce Readout Feedback (RoFB), a test-time intervention that converts intermediate predictions into token-wise pairwise coupling forces injected into the latent dynamics. Across three recurrent models (AKOrN, ItrSA++, TRM) on Sudoku and Maze, RoFB yields clear gains in four of six model-task pairs, achieving performance unattainable by merely running more steps or selecting from multiple trajectories, at comparable or lower computational cost. These results suggest that closed-loop steering of latent dynamics can serve as a complementary inference-time control mechanism for recurrent reasoning models.

</details>

Method: 提出一个双循环（double-loop）基于梯度的算法，用于学习对转移不确定性既高效又稳健的行为策略。算法通过双循环结构进行优化，理论上推导了新的转移方差梯度表达式，并建立了算法的全局收敛保证。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提方法相比现有方法对转移扰动更不敏感，验证了其在实践中的实用性。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效应对转移函数不确定性，学习出既高效又稳健的行为策略，为降低强化学习在线评估方差提供了理论保证和实证支持。

Abstract: In reinforcement learning policy evaluation, classic on-policy methods often suffer from high variance when estimating policy performance. To mitigate this issue, behavior policy search has been proposed to learn data-collecting policies tailored to reduce online evaluation variance. However, these approaches do not account for uncertainties in the transition functions. In practice, simulator transitions often differ from the real world due to modeling errors or approximation limitations. As a result, behavior policies trained in simulation may still yield high variance when deployed in real environments, leading to costly reliance on real-world evaluation samples. In this work, we propose a double-loop gradient-based algorithm for learning behavior policies that are both efficient and robust to transition uncertainty. Theoretically, we derive novel transition-variance gradient expressions and establish global convergence guarantees for the algorithm. Numerically, we demonstrate that our method is less sensitive to transition perturbations than existing approaches, providing supportive evidence for its practical utility.

</details>


### [44] [From Relaxed Indexability to Exact Indexability: A $t$-Step Approach for Partially Observable Restless Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24167)
*Qizhen Jia,Keqin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种t步前瞻阈值策略来近似部分可观测的restless多臂老虎机（RMAB）问题的Whittle指数。该方法通过t步有限时域值迭代定义活动-被动优势阈值，在t=1时退化为Liu[10]的线性阈值，在t>1时变为补贴依赖并更精确地逼近真实决策边界。作者证明了当原始问题满足Whittle指数可索引性时，t步近似Whittle指数以O(β^t)的几何速率收敛到精确Whittle指数。数值实验验证了2715个三状态实例的可索引性，P95索引误差从t=1时的2.18×10^-2降至t=8时的8.93×10^-4，运行时间随t温和增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Whittle索引策略是RMAB问题的可扩展方法，但在部分可观测性下，即使在单个信念点上确定无差异补贴也需要求解无闭合形式值函数的无限时域信念状态问题。Liu[10]通过线性化未知决策边界获得封闭形式的近似Whittle索引，但其阈值仅使用一步活动-被动比较，未考虑长时域延续价值，精度受限。

Method: 提出t步前瞻阈值策略：对每个补贴m，阈值由t步有限时域值迭代下的活动-被动优势定义。t>1时，阈值通过诱导的首穿结构变得补贴依赖。算法不需要可索引性作为输入，内置可索引性验证。在原始Whittle可索引性条件下，证明t步近似Whittle指数以O(β^t)几何收敛到精确值。

Result: 所有2715个三状态实例均通过可索引性验证。P95索引误差从t=1时的2.18×10^-2降至t=8时的8.93×10^-4。在β=0.9999的精确可比实例中，t=2已恢复精确Whittle索引排序。中等深度阈值策略优于一步基线并接近最优DP基准，运行时间随t温和增长。

Conclusion: t步前瞻阈值策略在部分可观测RMAB问题中提供了比一步线性化方法更精确的Whittle索引近似，具有几何收敛保证，且计算开销可控，是理论与实践之间良好的折中方案。

Abstract: Whittle index policies offer a scalable method for restless multi-armed bandits, but under partial observability even determining the indifference subsidy at a single belief requires solving an infinite-horizon belief-state problem with no closed-form value function. Liu [10] addresses this difficulty by linearizing the unknown decision boundary, leading to a linear system and a closed-form approximate Whittle index. However, the resulting threshold uses only a one-step active--passive comparison and does not account for longer-horizon continuation values.
  We extend this framework to a \emph{$t$-step lookahead threshold policy}. For each subsidy $m$, the threshold is defined by the active-minus-passive advantage under $t$-step finite-horizon value iteration. At $t=1$, the threshold is $m$-independent and recovers the linear threshold of Liu [10]; for $t>1$, it becomes subsidy-dependent through the induced first-crossing structure and tracks the exact decision boundary more closely. The proposed algorithm does not require indexability as an input and includes an indexability verification. Under the original Whittle indexability, we prove that the $t$-step approximate Whittle index converges geometrically to the exact Whittle index, \[ |\widehat W_t(ω)-W(ω)|=O(β^t). \] Numerically, all 2,715 tested three-state instances are verified as indexable according to the proposed criterion. The P95 index error decreases from $2.18\times10^{-2}$ at $t=1$ to $8.93\times10^{-4}$ at $t=8$. In an exact-comparable instance with $β=0.9999$, $t=2$ already recovers the exact Whittle-index ordering. Moderate-depth threshold policies also outperform the one-step baseline and remain close to the optimal dynamic-programming benchmark, while runtime grows mildly with $t$.

</details>


### [45] [PRQ-KMeans: Projection Residual Quantization for Semantic ID Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24207)
*Yunxiao Luo,Siyuan Wang,Ben Chen,Chenyi Lei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRQ-KMeans，一种改进残差量化标记器的后处理方法，通过去除全局均值分量、Top-k相似度加权质心更新和投影残差设计，解决了语义标识符构造中的三个局限，在工业搜索数据和推荐基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的残差量化标记器在构造语义标识符时存在三个关键局限：1) 语料库级别的共享分量会消耗第一层容量；2) 硬分配忽略了与邻近码字的渐变相似性；3) 全码字减法会在后续残差中残留沿所选码字方向的变化。这些限制影响了生成式检索和推荐系统的性能。

Method: 提出PRQ-KMeans方法，包含三个核心改进：(1) 去除全局均值分量，避免其占用第一层量化容量；(2) 使用Top-k相似度加权更新质心，替代传统硬分配，捕捉表示的渐变相似性；(3) 用投影残差替代全码字减法，移除每个表示沿所选质心方向的分量，避免后续残差中残留冗余信息。该方法工作在后处理（post-hoc）设置下，不依赖输入重构约束。

Result: 在大规模工业搜索数据集和四个公开推荐基准上，PRQ-KMeans在所有评估的标记器中取得最强的整体性能，在工业数据集上HitRate提升最高达7.4%，MRR提升最高达11.8%。

Conclusion: PRQ-KMeans通过解决残差量化中的公共分量移除、相似度建模和残差方向偏差问题，为生成式检索和推荐中的语义标识符构造提供了更优解决方案，验证了"渐进式公共性移除"视角的有效性。

Abstract: Semantic identifiers (SIDs) represent entities as hierarchical token sequences for generative retrieval and recommendation. Residual-quantization tokenizers construct these sequences by selecting a codeword at each level and passing a residual to the next. We view this process as progressive commonality removal: each token captures a component shared within its group, while later tokens should model the remaining differences. This view reveals three limitations: a corpus-wide shared component can consume first-level capacity, hard assignment ignores graded similarities to nearby codewords, and full-codeword subtraction can leave variation along the selected-codeword direction in the next residual. We therefore develop our solution in the post-hoc setting, where residual construction is not constrained by input reconstruction. Specifically, we propose PRQ-KMeans, which removes the global-mean component, refines centroids with Top-k similarity-weighted updates, and replaces full-codeword subtraction with a projection residual that removes each representation's selected-centroid component. Experiments on a large-scale industrial search dataset and four public recommendation benchmarks show that PRQ-KMeans achieves the strongest overall performance among the evaluated tokenizers, including gains of up to 7.4% in HitRate and 11.8% in MRR on the industrial dataset.

</details>


### [46] [A Data-dependent Early Stopping Rule using Rademacher Complexity with L1-norm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24210)
*Duy Hoang,Bastien Berret,Olivier Bruneau,Laurent Fribourg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Rademacher复杂度（RC）的解析框架，在不进行训练的情况下估计线性回归模型的最优早停时间。与基于随机矩阵理论的方法不同，该方法不需要对数据分布或协方差矩阵特征值分布作概率假设，并通过"线性探测"方法将结果推广到非线性神经网络（如MNIST分类）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练神经网络需要在拟合训练数据与泛化能力之间取得平衡，现有方法通常通过梯度下降训练和早停策略来数值估计泛化误差。虽然已有一些解析估计早停时间的工作，但它们基于随机矩阵理论，需要对数据分布或协方差矩阵特征值分布作假设。作者希望提出一种无需这些概率假设的解析框架。

Method: 作者提出基于Rademacher复杂度（RC）的解析框架来估计线性模型（线性回归）的最优早停时间。与通常使用L2范数不同，作者在理论和数值上都论证了使用L1范数表达RC更合适。此外，利用"线性探测"方法，将该框架成功扩展到非线性神经网络场景。

Result: 该框架无需训练即可解析估计最优早停时间，并且在MNIST分类实验中，通过线性探测方法成功应用于非线性神经网络，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于Rademacher复杂度（以L1范数表达）的解析框架，为估计最优早停时间提供了一种无需训练、无需概率分布假设的新途径，且可通过线性探测推广到非线性神经网络。

Abstract: Training neural networks requires balancing the trade-off between fitting the training data and achieving robust performance on unseen inputs. This ability, commonly referred to as generalizability, is determined by the gap between the empirical risk on the training set (``empirical loss'') and the expected risk over the data distribution (``generalization error''). Existing approaches typically estimate the generalization error numerically, requiring gradient descent training and an ``early stopping'' strategy. In this work, we introduce an analytic framework that estimates the optimal time of early stopping without the need for training. Several works in the literature also give such analytical estimations, but they are generally based on random matrix theory and often make assumptions on the distribution of the data or the eigenvalue distribution of the covariance matrix. In contrast, our work is based on Rademacher complexity (RC) without needing such probabilistic assumptions. For both theoretical and numerical reasons, it is more relevant to express RC with the L1- norm rather than with the L2-norm. We focus on the case of linear models and the problem of linear regression. Thanks to the ``linear probing'' method, our results can, however, be successfully applied to nonlinear neural networks, as illustrated in the classification MNIST example.

</details>


### [47] [Contrastive Branch Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24300)
*Ying Wang,Changlin Qiu,Bang Lin,Linbo Jin,Wen Jiang,Zhe Sun,Jingli Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出对比分支策略优化（CBPO），一种无需过程级标注的细粒度信用分配方法，用于工具集成智能体的强化学习训练，在十个基准上优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）虽能使语言模型学会与外部工具的多轮交互，但其稀疏的结果奖励无法提供信号来识别哪些中间决策导致了成功。现有分支采样方法虽可在替代续写之间产生局部比较，但往往混淆了两个不同的问题：分配固定探索预算和将分支结果转化为token级信用。

Method: CBPO将上述两个问题解耦并为每个问题设计专门机制。生成熵用于筛选整个响应中的候选分支位置，路径级和节点级衰减将固定预算分配到轨迹和位置，防止探索坍缩到少数路径或相邻token上。父轨迹与共享相同token前缀的分支构成精确前缀组，组内奖励变化定义对比分支价值（CBV），这是一种基于结果的局部决策敏感性估计，可重新缩放续写优势而不改变其符号。当同一轨迹上选择多个节点时，CBPO将其划分为不重叠的信用段，避免共享token上的重复梯度。

Result: 在十个基准上（五个数学推理、五个知识密集型搜索）的实验表明，CBPO在两个域和两种模型规模上均一致优于最先进的策略优化和基于分支的方法，取得了最高的宏平均准确率。

Conclusion: CBPO仅需结果奖励而无须过程级标注，为工具集成智能体训练中的细粒度信用分配提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) enables language models to learn multi-turn interaction with external tools, yet its sparse outcome rewards provide no signal for identifying which intermediate decisions are responsible for success. Branch sampling induces local comparisons among alternative continuations, but existing methods tend to conflate two distinct problems: allocating a fixed rollout budget and translating branch outcomes into token-level credit. We introduce Contrastive Branch Policy Optimization (CBPO), which disentangles these two problems and assigns a dedicated mechanism to each. Generation entropy screens candidate branch positions across the entire response, while path-level and node-level decay distribute a fixed budget across trajectories and positions to prevent exploration from collapsing onto a few paths or adjacent tokens. A parent trajectory together with the branches that share an identical token prefix forms an exact-prefix group, and the reward variation within this controlled group defines the Contrastive Branch Value (CBV), an outcome-based estimate of local decision sensitivity that rescales continuation advantages without altering their sign. When multiple nodes are selected along the same trajectory, CBPO partitions it into non-overlapping credit segments, thereby avoiding duplicated gradients on shared tokens. Requiring only outcome rewards and no process-level annotation, CBPO provides a practical solution for fine-grained credit assignment in tool-integrated agent training. Extensive experiments on ten benchmarks, including five for mathematical reasoning and five for knowledge-intensive search, show that CBPO consistently outperforms state-of-the-art policy-optimization and branch-based methods, attaining the highest macro-average accuracy in both domains and across two model scales.

</details>

*Mathis Jander,Wouter van Heeswijk,Martijn Mes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种因果分析框架，通过干预参数化合成时间序列生成器并在其他条件不变（ceteris paribus）条件下测量模型输出变化，来识别时序基础模型在部署前的偏差和失败模式。作者将该框架应用于Chronos-2和TimesFM-2.5，在六种不同的时间序列模式下测试，发现两种模型对趋势和调和振荡模式是安全的，但都存在高估持续性的偏差、对制度切换模式的突然失败，TimesFM-2.5还无法处理能量释放模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从定制化时间序列模型转向时序基础模型，使模型与应用的对应关系从一对一变为一对多。这种转变引入了集中化风险：许多潜在高风险的预测应用共享同一个模型的偏差和失败模式。因此，需要在大规模部署之前系统地识别这些模型的潜在缺陷，而现有研究缺乏系统性评估框架。

Method: 构建因果分析框架：通过干预参数化的合成时间序列生成器，在其他条件不变的情况下测量模型输出的相应变化，从而检验时序基础模型保持时间序列模式的能力。作者将该框架应用于Chronos-2和TimesFM-2.5两个模型，并在六个不同的时间序列模式（趋势、调和振荡、持久性、制度切换、能量释放等）上进行了测试。

Result: 研究发现：两种模型在对趋势和调和振荡模式的处理上是安全的；两种模型都存在高估持续性的偏差；两种模型在面对制度切换模式时都出现突然失败；TimesFM-2.5在能量释放模式下出现失败。作者对两个模型的原始论文进行审阅后发现，这些发现可能与预训练所用数据有关。

Conclusion: 研究结论包括：为时序基础模型的进一步开发提供建议、针对具体应用进行模型选择的推荐，以及讨论研究局限和未来研究方向。该框架为时序基础模型部署前的安全性验证提供了一种可操作的方法论。

Abstract: Transitioning from bespoke time series models towards time series foundation models changes the relationship of model and application from one-to-one to one-to-many. This shift introduces concentration risk as many, potentially high-risk, forecasting applications are exposed to the same biases and failure modes of a single time series foundation model. At the same time, this centralization allows for economies of scale in model development and validation. In this study we investigate how biases and failure modes of time series foundation models can be identified before deployment. We propose a causal analysis framework to investigate the ability of a time series foundation model to preserve time series patterns. To achieve this, we intervene on parameterized synthetic time series generators and measure the corresponding change in model output under ceteris paribus conditions. We apply our causal analysis framework to Chronos-2 and TimesFM-2.5 and test them across six distinct time series patterns. We find safe configurations for trend and harmonic oscillation patterns. The results also indicate a bias in both models towards overestimating persistence, sudden failures for both models against the regime switch pattern and failure for TimesFM-2.5 against the energy-release pattern. Our review of the original works for both models indicates that the findings might be explained by the data used for pretraining. We conclude our study with suggestions for further model development, recommendations for application-specific model selection, and a discussion of limitations and further research directions.

</details>

Method: 提出因子隐马尔可夫模型（FHMM）的结构化变体：将患者潜在健康状态分解为多个独立且同步演化的隐藏组件，每个组件对应合并症和实验室结果相关维度，随后利用该结构化潜在表示进行患者状态识别和疾病轨迹聚类。

Result: 模型在IQVIA医学研究数据（2006-2019年间首次使用非胰岛素抗糖尿病药物（NIAD）的T2DM患者记录）上识别出多个与已知糖尿病并发症模式一致的临床潜在组件，并揭示了异质性的进展路径，包括微血管主导型和多器官型轨迹，后者与更高的合并症负担和死亡率相关。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效捕捉电子健康记录（EHR）中有意义的纵向结构，为T2DM及其合并症的演变提供可解释的临床洞察，有助于识别高风险亚群和个性化疾病管理。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a structural variant of the Factorial Hidden Markov Model (FHMM) for the analysis of disease trajectories in patients with Type 2 diabetes mellitus (T2DM). The model represents a patient's latent health state as a combination of multiple independent, simultaneously evolving components, associated with comorbidities and lab results. This structured latent representation facilitates the identification of clinically meaningful patient states and clustering of common disease trajectories. We evaluate the proposed approach using The IQVIA Medical Research Data incorporating data from THIN, a Cegedim database of anonymized electronic health records (EHR), identifying patients with a first-ever prescription for a non-insulin antidiabetic drug (NIAD) between January 2006 and December 2019. The model identifies multiple clinically coherent latent components corresponding to known patterns of diabetes-related complications and reveals heterogeneous progression pathways, including distinct microvascular-dominant and multi-organ trajectories associated with elevated comorbidity burden and mortality. These results demonstrate that the proposed framework captures meaningful longitudinal structure in EHR data and provides interpretable insights into the evolution of T2DM and its comorbidities.

</details>


### [50] [When Does Self-Supervised Pretraining Help Tabular Models? A Study of Label Scarcity and Missing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24381)
*Sahand Mazrouei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了掩码-恢复自监督预训练目标在表格数据上的表现，覆盖14个分类任务，发现SSL在平均性能上优于从头训练、在测试时缺失场景下更稳健，但所有优势在统计显著性和多假设校正后均不显著，且改进程度因任务而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习作为表格数据的一种有前景的方法已被提出，但其在极端标签稀缺和测试时数据缺失场景下的有效性尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 在14个多样分类任务上，将掩码-恢复SSL预训练目标与从头训练及经典基线（如随机森林）进行比较；进一步在测试时注入MCAR和MNAR两种缺失机制评估鲁棒性；并在相同编码器架构下与VIME、SCARF、SubTab三种既有表格SSL基线进行对比。

Result: SSL平均优于从头训练（~0.8954 AUC vs. RF的0.9015），但差异不显著（p=0.626）；SSL在干净数据集上提升最可靠，在天然高缺失率数据集上反而常导致性能下降；测试时缺失下SSL平均AUC更高（MCAR +0.0245，MNAR +0.0418），但经Holm-Bonferroni校正后均不显著；与VIME、SCARF、SubTab无显著差异。

Conclusion: 表格SSL的收益在统计上缺乏显著性且高度依赖任务特性，与既有SSL基线的无差异表明这些发现反映了表格SSL的一般性质，而非特定预训练任务的独有现象。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) has emerged as a promising approach for tabular data, yet its efficacy under extreme label scarcity and test-time missingness remains under-explored. In this paper, we evaluate a mask-and-recover SSL pretraining objective against training from scratch and classical baselines across 14 diverse classification tasks. First, while SSL outperforms training from scratch on average and remains competitive with state-of-the-art tree ensembles (achieving ~0.8954 AUC vs. Random Forest's 0.9015 at 10% labels), the SSL-vs-scratch gains exhibit high inter-task variance and lack significance (p = 0.626 at both 5% and 10% labels). Second, contrary to the hypothesis that missing-value imputation objectives universally benefit datasets with native missingness, SSL yields the most reliable improvements on clean datasets, while frequently degrading performance on datasets with high inherent missingness. Third, despite this training variance, SSL-pretrained models achieve a higher average AUC than scratch-trained models under both test-time missingness completely at random (MCAR) injection (+0.0245 AUC, positive on 11 of 14 tasks) and structured missingness shifts (MNAR, +0.0418 AUC, positive on 8 of 14 tasks), though neither difference remains statistically significant after Holm-Bonferroni correction for multiple comparisons (adjusted p = 0.118 and p = 0.518, respectively). Fourth, comparing our mask-and-recover objective against three established tabular SSL baselines (VIME, SCARF, SubTab) under an identical encoder architecture, we find no significant difference from any of them (adjusted p = 0.459, p = 1.000, p = 1.000), indicating our findings reflect general properties of tabular SSL rather than idiosyncrasies of one particular pretext task.

</details>


### [51] [Equivariant Covariance Tensors: Guaranteed SPD Uncertainty for Tensor-Valued Geometric Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24386)
*Ruihan Liu,Yu Ji,Jianbo Yu,Shifu Yan,Qingchao Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向E(3)等变网络的不确定性量化（UQ）框架，针对对称二阶张量预测，同时建模均值和6×6协方差矩阵，通过不可约表示分解和矩阵指数映射确保旋转对称性与正定性，并引入基于多元Laplace分布的Log-Euclidean等变评分目标（LE-ESO）作为稳健损失函数，在ModelNet40和Materials Project数据集上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量值预测是几何深度学习的基础，但此类输出的不确定性量化（UQ）仍是开放挑战。现有的E(3)等变神经网络擅长点估计，却缺乏严格的置信度度量，无法为高风险物理场景提供可靠的不确定性信息。

Method: 1) 将全协方差矩阵分解为不可约表示Sym²(ρ_c) ≅ 2×(l=0) ⊕ 2×(l=2) ⊕ 1×(l=4)，确保协方差在旋转下保持等变；2) 通过从平坦Lie代数sym(6)到SPD流形的矩阵指数映射，严格保证协方差正定性并保持精确等变性；3) 提出Log-Euclidean等变评分目标（LE-ESO），基于多元Laplace分布的稳健替代损失，对重尾误差具有鲁棒性且优化稳定。

Result: 在ModelNet40惯性张量和Materials Project介电张量上的实验表明，该方法达到竞争性预测性能，同时提供物理一致、保持对称性的不确定性估计，对风险和分布外（OOD）检测具有良好的灵敏度。

Conclusion: 该框架首次系统性地解决了对称二阶张量预测中的E(3)等变不确定性量化问题，兼顾数学严谨性（正定性、等变性）与实用鲁棒性（重尾误差处理），为几何深度学习的UQ提供了新范式，有望推广至更高阶张量的不确定性建模。

Abstract: Tensor-valued prediction is fundamental to geometric deep learning, yet uncertainty quantification (UQ) for such outputs remains an open challenge. While E(3)-equivariant neural networks excel at point estimates, they lack rigorous confidence measures. We focus on symmetric rank-2 tensor prediction, where the target has six Kelvin--Mandel coordinates and full uncertainty is represented by a $6\times6$ covariance matrix. We introduce a framework for E(3)-equivariant UQ, modeling the full predictive distribution where both mean and covariance preserve rotational symmetry. Our approach decomposes the covariance into irreducible representations $\mathrm{Sym}^2(ρ_c) \cong 2\times(l=0) \oplus 2\times(l=2) \oplus 1\times(l=4)$. By mapping from the flat Lie algebra $\mathfrak{sym}(6)$ to the curved SPD manifold via matrix exponentiation, we strictly ensure positive-definite covariances while maintaining exact equivariance. Furthermore, we formulate a Log-Euclidean Equivariant Scoring Objective (LE-ESO)---a robust surrogate loss based on the Multivariate Laplace distribution---providing robustness to heavy-tailed errors and stable optimization. Validation on ModelNet40 inertia tensors and Materials Project dielectric tensors demonstrates that our method achieves competitive performance and provides physically consistent, symmetry-preserving uncertainty estimates with useful risk and OOD sensitivity.

</details>


### [52] [Joint Distribution Alignment for Universal Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24429)
*Shizhe Li,Hongshan Pu,Mengying Xie,Yi Xiang,Xiaowei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对通用域适应（UniDA）问题，首次从理论上给出了泛化误差上界，并据此提出JAUA算法，通过联合分布对齐和渐进式伪标签方法处理源域与目标域标签空间不一致的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统UDA假设源域与目标域标签空间完全相同，但实际应用中标签空间可能存在差异（即UniDA场景），而现有工作很少对该场景提供理论分析。

Method: 1）推导通用域适应的泛化误差上界；2）基于该上界提出JAUA算法，通过最小化卡方散度计算的分布差异来对齐联合分布；3）提出渐进式伪标签方法为目标域无标签样本分配伪标签。

Result: 在六个公开图像数据集上的实验结果表明，JAUA在解决UniDA问题上具有优越性。

Conclusion: JAUA通过理论驱动的联合分布对齐策略，有效解决了通用域适应中标签空间差异与分布漂移共存的挑战。

Abstract: Unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) has been widely concerned in the fields of machine learning, pattern recognition, and computer vision. Traditional UDA learning usually assumes that the label spaces of the source and target domains are exactly the same and only needs to solve the problem of sample distribution drift existing between two domains. However, in real world applications, the label spaces between two domains may be different. In this case, there are both sample distribution drift and class spatial difference between domains, namely Universal Domain Adaptation (UniDA) learning scenario. At present, existing works rarely offer theoretical analysis for universal domain adaptation. In this paper, we provide an upper bound of the generalization error for universal domain adaptation. According to the proposed generalization error bound, we propose a novel UniDA algorithm called Joint Distribution Alignment for Universal Domain Adaptation (JAUA), which aligns the joint distributions by minimizing the distribution discrepancy calculated by Chi-Square divergence. Furthermore, we propose a progressive pseudo-labeling method to assign the pseudo labels to unlabeled target samples. The experiment results on six public image datasets demonstrate the superiority of JAUA in handling the UniDA problem.

</details>


### [53] [Evaluating Deep Multivariate Imputation Models on Wearable Device Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24436)
*Skye Goodman,Roussel Desmond Nzoyem,Leandro Junges,Peter Kissack,Yasser Qureshi,Amberly Brigden,Jeff Clark,Nawid Keshtmand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对可穿戴设备多模态生理数据中的结构化缺失问题（共享物理传感器的特征同时丢失），提出了一种从训练数据中挖掘连续缺失模板并按特征间隔分位数分层的块状掩码评估协议，同时提出匹配的训练协议。在单参与者癫痫患者Garmin智能手表数据上，该训练协议使BRITS严重缺失MAE降低43%，并发现不同模型在不同缺失场景下各有优势，模型排名显著依赖于评估设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习插补方法（如BRITS、SAITS）在真实缺失模式下的多模态生理数据上评估有限；传统基准采用随机点式保留协议，错误假设缺失在特征和时间维度上独立，无法反映可穿戴数据中特征因共享物理传感器而同时丢失的结构化缺失特性，导致模型真实能力被掩盖。

Method: 1）提出从训练数据中挖掘连续缺失模板的评估协议，按各特征间隔长度分位数分层，以保留共缺失结构的块状掩码注入数据；2）提出匹配训练协议，使模型暴露于相同缺失分布；3）扩展BRITS，加入时间编码与昼夜谐波通道；4）在单参与者Garmin智能手表癫痫患者数据上，对线性插值、BRITS（扩展版）和SAITS进行系统比较评估。

Result: 匹配训练协议使BRITS在严重缺失间隔上的MAE降低43%；线性插值在短间隔的慢变特征上最优；扩展BRITS在中度与严重缺失间隔的动态心脏特征上MAE更低；SAITS在保持真实分布方面（以JS距离衡量）更优但MAE更高。模型排名强烈依赖于评估设计。

Conclusion: 传统随机点式评估会掩盖模型真实能力，所提出的可迁移协议为未来多传感器可穿戴数据集的插补策略开发奠定了关键基础，并表明针对不同缺失场景选择合适模型而非追求单一最优模型更为实用。

Abstract: Wearable device data enables continuous health monitoring, but suffers from structured missingness: features sharing a physical sensor drop out together. Deep imputation methods such as BRITS and SAITS have seen limited evaluation on multimodal physiological data under realistic missingness, and existing benchmarks use random-point holdout protocols that incorrectly assume missingness is independent across features and time. Using data from a person with epilepsy recorded on a Garmin smartwatch, we develop an evaluation protocol that mines contiguous missing-run templates from training data, stratifies them by per-feature gap-length quantiles, and injects them as block masks with preserved co-missingness structure. A matched training protocol exposing models to the same missingness distribution reduces BRITS's severe-gap MAE by 43%, demonstrating the potential benefit of the proposed evaluation and training protocol within this single-participant dataset. We further extend BRITS with time-of-day encoding and a circadian harmonic channel. No single model dominates: linear interpolation is optimal for slow-moving features over short gaps; extended BRITS achieves lower MAE on dynamic cardiac features in moderate and severe gaps; and SAITS better preserves the ground-truth distribution by Jensen-Shannon distance despite higher MAE. Ultimately, model rankings strongly depend on evaluation designs. By exposing how traditional evaluation methods obscure true model capabilities, our transferable protocol establishes critical steps towards developing better imputation strategies for future multi-sensor wearable datasets.

</details>


### [54] [WarpSAC: Towards the Pinnacle of Scalable Off-policy RL by Rethinking Exploration and Exploitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24479)
*Zihao Wu,Hongyao Tang,Yi Ma,Huizhong Song,Pengyi Li,Yifu Yuan,Fei Ni,Jinyi Liu,Wei Wei,Jianrong Wang,Yan Zheng,Jianye Hao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WarpSAC，一种数据规模感知的off-policy强化学习算法族。通过八个基准家族的对照实验，发现RL稳定器（如参数归一化、clipped double-Q）的效果依赖数据规模：数据稀少时参数归一化有帮助，数据充足时反而限制价值拟合。WarpSAC提供两个变体（WarpSAC-L和WarpSAC-A）分别适配CPU有限数据训练和GPU高吞吐并行训练，在多个基准上显著超越FlashSAC，成功率从19.8%提升至96.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模并行仿真彻底改变了off-policy RL的训练数据格局，但现有的稳定器（如参数归一化、clipped double-Q）是为数据受限的传统replay设计，在数据充裕的新场景下可能不再适用甚至有害。作者旨在系统性地研究稳定器在不同数据规模下的行为，并据此设计适应数据规模的算法。

Method: （1）在八个基准家族中进行受控实验，系统比较各稳定器（参数归一化、clipped double-Q、age-biased replay weighting）在不同数据规模下的表现；（2）基于发现提出WarpSAC算法族：核心采用Sample Weight Decay（样本权重衰减）来实现高效利用，并衍生两个变体——WarpSAC-L（开启参数归一化且使用clipped double-Q）面向数据受限的CPU训练，WarpSAC-A（关闭参数归一化且使用单Q）面向数据充裕的GPU并行训练。

Result: WarpSAC在9个CPU规模环境中比FlashSAC提升normalized score-step AUC 4.5%，在14个GPU并行环境中提升23.1%。UnitreeG1TransportBox-v1成功率达96.4%（对比FlashSAC的19.8%），MuJoCo Playground上mean normalized wall-time AUC提升19.1%，Unitree G1的sim-to-real部署速度提升36.4%。

Conclusion: 可扩展的off-policy RL应根据可用数据规模来自适应地调整其稳定器。不同的数据规模需要不同的稳定化策略，固定不变的稳定器设计会限制算法的扩展能力。

Abstract: Massively parallel simulation changes the data regime in which off-policy reinforcement learning (RL) is trained, challenging stabilizers designed for data-limited replay. Through controlled experiments across eight benchmark families, we show that these stabilizers are data-regime-dependent: parameter normalization helps with narrow replay coverage but restricts value fitting when data are abundant, while clipped double-Q can be relaxed in high-throughput manipulation. Age-biased replay weighting improves learning efficiency across regimes, especially with limited network capacity.
  Based on these findings, we propose WarpSAC, a regime-aware family of off-policy RL algorithms. WarpSAC uses Sample Weight Decay for efficient exploitation and provides two variants: WarpSAC-L (Norm ON, clipped double-Q) for data-limited CPU-scale training, and WarpSAC-A (Norm OFF, single-Q) for data-abundant GPU-parallel training. WarpSAC improves normalized score--step AUC over FlashSAC by 4.5% across nine CPU-scale environments and 23.1% across fourteen GPU-parallel environments. It increases UnitreeG1TransportBox-v1 success rate from 19.8% to 96.4%, improves mean normalized wall-time AUC on MuJoCo Playground by 19.1%, and achieves 36.4% faster sim-to-real deployment on Unitree G1 than FlashSAC. These results show that scalable off-policy RL should adapt its stabilizers to the available data regime.

</details>


### [55] [Beyond Static Interpretability: Anticipating Post-SFT Mechanisms from Pre-SFT Parameters for Better Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24482)
*Hang Chen,Jiaying Zhu,Wenya Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"前瞻性定位"框架，通过在SFT之前预测训练后模型的机制可解释性状态，利用泰勒展开建模参数演化，并在神经元和组件两个粒度上进行定位，从而解决了传统机制可解释性在指导SFT时因回溯性本质而产生的误导问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机制可解释性具有回溯性（retrospective）本质，仅能解释已有模型的状态。在"先定位后微调"（locating-then-tuning）范式中，直接对SFT前的模型进行解释会得出误导性结论——因为对于新任务而言，初始识别的关键神经元与最终微调后模型的决策神经元截然不同，这些偏差会主动干扰SFT过程，导致微调效果恶化。

Method: 1) 理论上：将SFT建模为连续的参数演化过程，利用泰勒展开严格连接微调后的机制目标与SFT前模型的动态梯度信息；2) 实践上：设计双粒度定位流水线，包括神经元级（neuron-level）和组件级（component-level）两个层次的定位，仅使用SFT前参数和目标数据集即可准确估计SFT后的可解释性状态。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法不仅提供更优的SFT指导效果，还展现出鲁棒性能，并且随着模型规模增大具有良好的时间可扩展性（temporal scalability）。

Conclusion: 该工作超越了传统可解释性的根本局限——即无法在任务关键机制被训练之前识别它们，开创了将机制可解释性与目标优化统一起来的预测性前沿。

Abstract: Mechanistic Localization bridges mechanistic interpretability and post-training optimization by isolating critical parameters via interpretative approaches and then guiding parameter-efficient Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) in a ``locating-then-tuning'' paradigm. However, due to the retrospective nature of mechanistic interpretability, directly interpreting pre-SFT models introduces misleading conclusions. Specifically for novel tasks, initially identified neurons differ drastically from those governing the final model, introducing biases that actively disrupt SFT. To address this, we propose a forward-looking localization framework that accurately estimates the post-SFT interpretability state using only pre-SFT parameters and the target dataset. Theoretically, we model SFT as a continuous parameter evolution, leveraging Taylor expansion to rigorously bridge the post-tuning mechanistic objective with the pre-SFT model's dynamic gradients. Practically, we design dual-granularity (neuron- and component-level) localization pipelines. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach not only provides superior SFT guidance but also exhibits robust performance and temporal scalability across increasing model sizes. This work transcends the fundamental limitation of traditional interpretability-its inability to identify task-critical mechanisms before they are trained-pioneering a predictive frontier that unites mechanistic interpretability with targeted optimization.

</details>


### [56] [Where Entropy Is Measured Matters: Policy Geometry in Bounded Continuous-Control PPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24488)
*Yiyang He,Zhichun Zhou,Ziwei Wang,Tao Xue,Haolin Fei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了PPO中熵测量位置（潜在高斯熵H(u) vs 执行动作熵H(a)）对连续控制有界策略几何的影响。实验表明，在80肌肉MyoLeg任务中，裁剪高斯策略有89.07%的动作落在边界5%范围内，且该现象并非仅由方差引起（方差置零仍保留83.83%），因为82.12%的状态条件均值超出可执行区间。latent熵对均值梯度为零、对方差全是增大的梯度，而executed-action熵通过Jacobian向均值施加向内梯度，显著降低边界占用率（71.42% vs 18.83%）。38维Dog-Stand任务的独立PPO实现复现了该排序。均值惩罚可匹配或超越H(a)的居中效果，但相同均值几何仍可伴随不同方差和回报，说明熵测量空间是均值-方差耦合的设计选择，任务回报单独无法表征有界策略几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续控制策略常被优化为无界高斯分布再映射到有界动作空间，但熵测量位置（潜在空间还是执行空间）对策略几何和RL训练的影响尚未被系统研究。作者观察到高边界占用率现象，旨在揭示熵测量空间如何与动作边界交互、影响均值-方差耦合的优化动态。

Method: 采用PPO在多任务上进行系统实验：(1) 通过同状态分解（zero-variance消融）分离方差与均值对边界占用的贡献；(2) 比较clipping与tanh两种有界化映射；(3) 形式化推导H(u)与H(a)在均值和方差上的梯度差异；(4) 在80肌肉MyoLeg与38维Dog-Stand两个任务、多个随机种子及独立CleanRL实现下进行复现；(5) 设计直接均值惩罚作为对照，并对匹配的均值几何下方差与回报解耦进行分析。

Result: 裁剪高斯策略中89.07%的动作落在边界5%内；方差置零仍留83.83%边界占用，82.12%的均值出界。H(u)对均值零梯度、对方差恒定增大梯度；H(a)通过Jacobian产生均值向内梯度。三个匹配种子下，latent熵、无熵、executed熵的边界占用率分别为71.42%、29.76%和18.83%。Dog-Stand独立复现确认均值几何排序，且在共享状态评估和1%-10%边界裕度下均成立。直接均值惩罚可匹配甚至超过H(a)的居中效果，表明内部均值并非executed熵独有。

Conclusion: 熵测量空间构成有界策略的一个耦合均值-方差设计选择：在潜在空间测熵会倾向于将动作质量集中于边界附近，而在执行空间测熵则有助于生成内部均值。任务回报单维度不足以刻画有界策略的几何特性，必须将熵测量位置视为与策略参数化同等重要的设计维度。

Abstract: Many continuous-control policies are optimized as unbounded Gaussians and then mapped into bounded actions. We show that where entropy is measured changes the policy geometry learned by proximal policy optimization (PPO). In an 80-muscle MyoLeg task, a clipped Gaussian executes 89.07% of actions within 5% of a bound. A same-state decomposition shows that this is not due to variance alone: setting variance to zero still leaves 83.83% of actions near a bound, while 82.12% of state-conditioned means lie outside the executable interval. Replacing clipping with a tanh map does not remove the high-variance regime. For latent Gaussian entropy H(u), the entropy loss has zero gradient with respect to the mean and a constant variance-increasing gradient. For executed-action entropy H(a), the transform Jacobian adds an inward gradient on the mean. Across three matched MyoLeg seeds, near-boundary occupancy is 71.42%, 29.76%, and 18.83% under latent entropy, no entropy, and executed-action entropy. A 38-dimensional Dog-Stand replication with an independent CleanRL-based PPO implementation reproduces the ordering in mean geometry, which also survives shared-state evaluation and boundary margins from 1% to 10%. Direct mean penalties can match or exceed the centering produced by H(a), showing that interior means are not unique to executed entropy. However, matched mean geometry can coexist with substantially different variance and return. Entropy measurement space is therefore a coupled mean-variance design choice, and task return alone does not characterize bounded-policy geometry.

</details>


### [57] [When Do Supervised UQ Ensembles Improve LLM Hallucination Detection? A Robustness Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24492)
*Mohit Singh Chauhan,Vipin Gyanchandani,Dylan Bouchard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了大语言模型中基于监督集成的不确定性量化（UQ）方法用于幻觉检测的鲁棒性。在4个LLM、9个数据集和3种生成模式下，监督集成在32个设置中的30个优于最佳单一评分器，仅需最少100个标注样本即可获得收益。研究还发现基于采样的黑盒集成效果接近完整集成，而单次生成的白盒集成收益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究提出了通过学习式集成组合多种UQ信号来提升幻觉检测性能，但缺乏对这些集成方法鲁棒性的系统性实证考察。作者旨在填补这一空白，从样本效率、域内数据集迁移和生成模式依赖性三个维度全面评估监督集成框架的稳健性。

Method: 采用监督集成框架：在小型域特定标注LLM响应数据集上训练一个分类器，整合异构UQ评分器的输出，然后将其应用于样本外的幻觉分类，无需检索、工具或参考文档。实验覆盖4个LLM、9个数据集、3种生成模式（短问答、长文生成、代码生成），并进行三维鲁棒性分析：样本效率（标注实例数量对性能的影响）、域内数据集迁移（分布偏移下的表现）、生成模式依赖性（不同任务类型的泛化能力）。对比了采样型黑盒集成与单次生成白盒集成。

Result: 监督集成在32个设置中的30个优于最佳单一评分器，且从最少100个标注样本即可获得性能提升。在域内迁移（分布偏移）场景下，集成在28个迁移设置中的23个优于最佳非集成评分器。采样型黑盒集成几乎达到完整集成的效果，而单次生成白盒集成的收益有限。

Conclusion: 监督集成方法在幻觉检测中具有显著的鲁棒性优势：样本效率高、在域内分布偏移下表现稳健、泛化能力强。黑盒采样集成是实用且高效的选择，而白盒单次生成集成价值有限。这些发现为实际部署中如何选择UQ集成策略提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) methods are widely used for hallucination detection in large language models (LLMs) in closed-book settings where ground-truth evidence is unavailable at inference time. Prior work has proposed combining UQ signals via learned ensembles, but empirical investigations into the robustness of these ensembles are limited. We study a supervised ensembling framework that trains a classifier over heterogeneous UQ-based scorer outputs on a small, domain-specific dataset of labeled LLM responses, then applies it to out-of-sample hallucination classification without retrieval, tools, or reference documents. Across four LLMs, nine datasets, and three generation regimes (short-form QA, long-form generation, and code generation), we provide a systematic robustness analysis along three axes: sample efficiency, in-domain dataset transfer, and generation regime dependence. We find that supervised ensembles outperform the best individual scorer in 30 of 32 settings, with gains realized from as few as 100 labeled instances. Ensembles retain most of their advantage in cases of in-domain transfer under distribution shift, outperforming the best non-ensemble scorer in 23 of 28 transfer settings. Sampling-based black-box ensembles are nearly as effective as full ensembles, while single-generation white-box ensembles offer limited benefit.

</details>


### [58] [It depends: Incorporating correlations for joint aleatoric and epistemic uncertainties of high-dimensional output spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24518)
*Leonhard F. Feiner,Manuel Nickel,Martin Menten,Laurin Lux,Rickmer Braren,Daniel Rueckert,Georgios Kaissis,Raphael Rehms,Johannes Paetzold*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的不确定性量化方法，将认知不确定性和偶然不确定性联合建模为统一的二阶分布，通过低秩加对角协方差结构近似高维输出空间中的联合不确定性，在图像修复、着色、光流和深度估计等任务中取得了优于现有方法的UQ性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维回归任务中（如医学图像分割、图像修复等），偶然不确定性捕捉数据固有的噪声，而认知不确定性量化模型在陌生条件下的置信度。单独建模其中一种不确定性会限制预测的透明度和鲁棒性，因此需要联合建模两种不确定性，但直接建模联合不确定性在计算上代价高昂。

Method: 提出一种新方法，使用低秩加对角协方差结构近似联合不确定性，捕获输出维度间的重要相关性同时避免全协方差矩阵的计算负担。方法将偶然不确定性和认知不确定性显式地结合为统一的二阶分布，支持下游分析（如采样和对数似然评估），并引入了稳定化策略以提升训练和推理效率。

Result: 在图像修复、颜色化、光流和深度估计等多个高维回归任务中取得了优于现有方法的不确定性量化性能，验证了联合建模两种不确定性及低秩近似结构的有效性。

Conclusion: 联合建模偶然不确定性和认知不确定性在高维回归任务中能够提供更可靠的不确定性估计，低秩加对角协方差结构在计算效率和表达力之间取得了良好平衡，为高维UQ提供了可行的统一框架。

Abstract: Uncertainty Quantification (UQ) plays a vital role in enhancing the reliability of deep learning model predictions, especially in scenarios with high-dimensional output spaces. This paper addresses the dual nature of uncertainty -- aleatoric and epistemic -- focusing on their joint integration in high-dimensional regression tasks. For example, in applications like medical image segmentation or restoration, aleatoric uncertainty captures inherent data noise, while epistemic uncertainty quantifies the model's confidence in unfamiliar conditions. Modeling both jointly enables more reliable predictions by reflecting both unavoidable variability and knowledge gaps, whereas modeling only one limits transparency and robustness. We propose a novel approach that approximates the resulting joint uncertainty using a low-rank plus diagonal covariance structure, capturing essential output correlations while avoiding the computational burdens of full covariance matrices. Unlike prior work, our method explicitly combines aleatoric and epistemic uncertainties into a unified second-order distribution that supports robust downstream analyses like sampling and log-likelihood evaluation. We further introduce stabilization strategies for efficient training and inference, achieving superior UQ in the tasks of image inpainting, colorization, optical flow, and depth estimation.

</details>


### [59] [From Numerical Simulators of PDEs to Neural Emulators and Back](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24547)
*Felix Koehler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文提出神经仿真器与经典PDE数值求解器并非对立范式，而是可通过模式级傅里叶分析建立统一语言相互借鉴。论文通过解构求解器在仿真器学习管线中的多重角色，贡献了三项成果：APEBench基准测试套件、渐进精化可微物理研究以及神经仿真器优越性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDE数值求解器成本高昂，急需快速神经仿真器；但神经仿真器常被视为训练数据来源（即数值求解器）的"黑箱替代品"，缺乏对两者关系的系统性理解。作者认为这两种范式其实高度相似：神经架构镜像经典离散化，误差可通过相同谱分析理解，知识可在两个方向流动。核心动机是澄清求解器在仿真器学习管线中的多重角色，从而建立桥梁。

Method: 论文采用模式级（mode-wise）傅里叶分析作为统一语言，同时刻画求解器误差、架构归纳偏置和训练目标。基于此框架，论文：（1）构建APEBench基准套件，利用JAX中快速可微伪谱求解器评测自回归神经仿真器；（2）提出渐进精化可微物理方法，研究未收敛求解器对代理模型训练的影响；（3）开展神经仿真器优越性分析，考察数值误差与架构归纳偏置的作用。

Result: 论文形成了三项综合贡献：APEBench为自回归神经PDE仿真器提供了全面的基准测试平台；渐进精化可微物理揭示了求解器未收敛状态对代理训练效果的影响机制；神经仿真器优越性分析阐明了数值误差与架构归纳偏置的相对贡献。通过傅里叶模式分析的通用框架，论文展现了两类范式之间的深层次一致性。

Conclusion: 论文论证了神经仿真器与数值求解器本质上是同一数学框架的两个侧面，通过傅里叶模式分析可建立双向的知识流通渠道。这一视角为神经仿真器的设计、基准评测和误差理解提供了新的理论基础，也为进一步改进仿真器训练管线指明了方向。

Abstract: Simulation is central to modern engineering and science, but the cost of numerical solvers for partial differential equations (PDEs) remains a bottleneck whenever fast or many-query evaluations are required. Neural emulators trained on solver-generated data promise significant speedups, yet they are usually framed as opaque alternatives to the very methods that produce their training signal. This thesis argues the two paradigms are more alike than different: neural architectures mirror classical discretizations, their errors are amenable to the same spectral analysis, and insight flows profitably in both directions. We approach the relationship by disentangling the multiple roles a solver plays in the emulator learning pipeline. Mode-wise Fourier analysis then provides a common language in which solver errors, architectural inductive biases, and training objectives can all be read off simultaneously. Taken together, this allows synthesizing three contributions. (1) APEBench, a comprehensive benchmarking suite for autoregressive neural emulators of PDEs that uses fast differentiable pseudo-spectral solvers in JAX. (2) Progressively Refined Differentiable Physics, an investigation of the effect of unconverged solvers on surrogate training. (3) Neural Emulator Superiority, an analysis of the influence of numerical errors and architectural inductive biases.

</details>


### [60] [Persistent Cross Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24549)
*Sijin Yeom,Jae-Hun Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了持久交叉熵（PCE），通过结合相似度函数与持久性加权构造诱导概率，将Shannon交叉熵推广到持久性图上，并证明了其稳定性质，在三个数值实验中展示了其区分同熵图、识别动力系统因果方向及作为知识蒸馏的方向性拓扑损失等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久熵是持久性图上基于概率测度的Shannon熵，但其交叉熵版本无法自然定义，因为两个持久性图的事件空间通常不同。需要一种方法桥接不同的事件空间，以扩展交叉熵到持久性图领域。

Method: 将相似度函数与持久性权重相结合，定义诱导概率，该概率从一个图的信息反映到另一个图的事件空间上，并将无法解释的概率质量分配给未解释事件。基于诱导概率将交叉熵扩展到持久性图，即持久交叉熵（PCE）。

Result: 建立了诱导概率和PCE的主要性质，并证明了二者的稳定性定理。三个数值实验表明：PCE能区分具有相同持久熵的图、无需构建联合持久图即可分离动力系统中的因果方向，并可作为知识蒸馏中的方向性拓扑损失。

Conclusion: 持久交叉熵为持久性图提供了一种有理论保证的、跨事件空间的熵度量工具，在拓扑数据分析中具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Persistent entropy is the Shannon entropy of a persistence-based probability measure defined on a persistence diagram. However, its cross-entropy version is not naturally defined because two persistence diagrams generally have different event spaces. To bridge these event spaces, we combine a similarity function with persistence weighting to define an induced probability. The induced probability reflects information from one diagram on the event space of the other diagram and assigns unexplained probability mass to the unexplained event. Using the induced probability, we extend cross entropy to persistence diagrams, called persistent cross entropy (PCE). We establish the main properties of both the induced probability and PCE and prove stability theorems for both. Through three numerical studies, we show that PCE distinguishes diagrams with the same persistent entropy, separates causal directions in dynamical systems without constructing a joint persistent diagram, and can be used as a directional topology loss for knowledge distillation.

</details>


### [61] [FraudBench: Protocol-Sensitive Benchmarking of Adversarial Robustness for Financial Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24551)
*Xitong Zeng,Zhaoge Bi,Yitian Yang,Huaming Chen,Quan Z. Sheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FraudBench是一个协议敏感的对抗鲁棒性基准测试平台，专门用于金融欺诈和信用风险检测场景。它通过三种匹配的评估协议（无约束攻击、事后可行性过滤、部署感知的约束集成攻击）在同一数据集-模型-攻击-防御设置下评估鲁棒性，证明评估协议本身会显著影响鲁棒性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融表格数据的对抗鲁棒性评估存在困难，因为涉及领域特定约束、严重类别不平衡和不对称攻击者能力。现有评估方法将领域约束作为事后有效性检查，忽略了约束与攻击过程交互对鲁棒性结论的深远影响，导致不同评价方式得出截然不同的鲁棒性结论。

Method: 提出FraudBench基准框架，在相同数据集-模型-攻击-防御设置下引入三种匹配的评估协议：(1)无约束攻击；(2)事后可行性过滤；(3)部署感知的约束集成攻击。覆盖四个公开金融数据集，评估神经网络、树模型和集成模型在三种攻击设置下的鲁棒性表现。

Result: 鲁棒性结论高度依赖评估协议。在Lending Club贷款数据白盒设置下，事后过滤平均仅留下3.7个可行翻转样本，而基于攻击内投影加攻击者可变性掩码的方法在同一扰动预算下产生了2,832.3个可行翻转样本。IEEE-CIS数据集的结果进一步表明可行性与攻击者能力是独立的评估维度，黑盒评估显示协议选择可以改变模型族排名。

Conclusion: 金融欺诈鲁棒性评估应同时报告预测性能下降和攻击可行性两个维度，并将领域约束集成到攻击生成过程中，而非将其作为后处理检查。该方法为金融领域对抗鲁棒性的标准化评估提供了重要框架。

Abstract: Machine learning models are widely used in financial fraud and credit-risk detection, yet their adversarial robustness remains difficult to evaluate because financial tabular data involve domain-specific constraints, severe class imbalance, and asymmetric attacker capability. We argue that, in this setting, robustness is not only an attribute of the model, but also an attribute of the evaluation protocol. Different ways of enforcing constraints and capability can lead to substantially different robustness conclusions. This paper presents FraudBench, a protocol-sensitive benchmark for adversarial robustness evaluation in financial fraud and credit-risk detection. Rather than treating domain constraints as post-hoc validity checks, FraudBench evaluates the same dataset--model--attack--defence setting under three matched protocols: unconstrained attacks, post-hoc feasibility filtering, and deployment-aware constraint-integrated attacks. FraudBench covers four public financial datasets, and evaluates neural, tree-based, and ensemble models using three attack settings. Our results show that robustness conclusions are highly protocol-sensitive. On Lending Club Loan Data under the white-box setting, post-hoc filtering leaves only 3.7 feasible-flipped examples on average, whereas in-attack projection with attacker mutability masking produces 2,832.3 feasible-flipped examples under the same perturbation budget. The results on IEEE-CIS further show that feasibility and attacker capability are separate axes, while black-box evaluation shows that protocol choice can alter model-family rankings. These findings suggest that fraud robustness evaluation should report predictive degradation and attack feasibility jointly, and should incorporate domain constraints into attack generation rather than treating them as post-processing checks.

</details>


### [62] [SeisMamba: Low-Latency Single-Station Seismic Magnitude Estimation for Spatially Distributed Earthquake Early Warning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24561)
*Quenton Yeo,Zhaoge Bi,Linghan Huang,Luke Stephen Higgins,Flora Salim,Huaming Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SeisMamba，一种基于Mamba的轻量级架构，用于单站地震波形的低延迟震级估计，在STEAD基准上取得最佳精度且比Transformer基线快约3倍，并在地理上的未见区域保持有效性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地震早期预警系统依赖密集区域地震台网和区域特定校准，导致高风险的稀疏感知基础设施区域存在空间覆盖障碍；单站学习方法虽成本较低，但现有模型常面临精度-延迟权衡，且在区域分布偏移下性能退化。

Method: SeisMamba采用Mamba架构，结合层次化卷积编码、稀疏选择性状态空间建模、多尺度特征融合，以及一个辅助时间预测头，支持高效的长序列波形分析，仅需最少预处理的三分量单站地震波形。

Result: 在STEAD基准上，SeisMamba在MSE、RMSE和R²指标上均优于所有基线，在NVIDIA T4 GPU上处理32条波形仅需0.55 ms，比基于Transformer的基线约快3倍；在智利-台湾区域保留实验中，对地理上未见的地震区域仍保持有效性能。

Conclusion: 选择性状态空间波形建模为空间分布的低成本地震早期预警提供了有前景的精度-延迟骨干架构。

Abstract: Rapid earthquake magnitude estimation is central to earthquake early warning, yet many operational systems depend on dense regional seismic networks and region-specific calibration. This creates a spatial coverage barrier for high-risk areas with sparse sensing infrastructure. Single-station learning offers a lower-cost alternative, but existing models often face an accuracy--latency trade-off and may degrade under regional distribution shift. We present SeisMamba, a lightweight Mamba-based architecture for low-latency magnitude estimation from minimally processed three-component seismic waveforms recorded at a single station. SeisMamba combines hierarchical convolutional encoding, sparse selective state-space modelling, multi-scale feature fusion, and an auxiliary temporal prediction head to support efficient long-sequence waveform analysis. On the STEAD benchmark, SeisMamba achieves the best MSE, RMSE, and $R^2$ among tested baselines while requiring only 0.55 ms for a batch of 32 waveforms on an NVIDIA T4 GPU, making it about three times faster than transformer-based baselines. We further conduct a Chile--Taiwan regional hold-out experiment as a diagnostic test of cross-region deployment, where SeisMamba retains useful performance on geographically unseen seismic regions. These results suggest that selective state-space waveform modelling provides a promising accuracy--latency backbone for spatially distributed, low-cost earthquake early warning.

</details>


### [63] [Across the Loss Landscape with Progressive Growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24568)
*Paul Caillon,Christophe Cerisara,Alexandre Allauzen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究增量式"增长-再优化"策略如何将训练偏向损失景观中更平坦区域。通过将增长视为逐步约束松弛（从低维子模型出发，逐步解锁嵌套随机子空间），作者理论上证明了渐进增长通过冻结约束诱导的"体积效应"增加了宽盆地权重、抑制了尖锐盆地，并在玩具景观和ResNet/CIFAR-100上验证了该预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在高度非凸、过参数化的损失景观上仍能良好泛化，这一现象常与随机优化找到的极小值点几何性质相关。已有研究多从优化器角度解释平坦极小值的偏好，但对"渐进式增长"（grow-and-optimize）策略如何系统性地将训练偏向平坦区域缺乏理论理解，因此作者希望从约束松弛的角度建立统一的理论框架。

Method: 将渐进增长建模为逐步约束松弛过程：从低维子模型开始，每次扩展时解锁嵌套随机子空间，同时将正交补空间冻结在初始化值，每步重新优化。理论上，在非退化极小值附近的标准局部正则条件下，证明局部子水平集可由椭球近似，并通过冻结方向上的显式有效曲率刻画盆地可达性。据此从体积效应角度解释渐进增长偏好宽盆地、抑制窄盆地的机理。实验上在受控玩具景观和ResNet/CIFAR-100上验证理论预测。

Result: 渐进子空间增长确实能够可靠地产生更平坦的解；但曲率降低并不总是转化为测试性能的提升，说明平坦性-泛化之间的关联存在微妙的边界条件。实验结果在玩具模型与真实网络（ResNet/CIFAR-100）上均与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 论文从约束松弛视角为渐进增长策略的平坦性偏好提供了严格的理论解释（体积效应），同时揭示了平坦性并不必然带来泛化收益，提醒研究者对"更平坦=更泛化"这一常见假设持审慎态度。

Abstract: Deep neural networks generalize well despite their highly nonconvex, overparameterized loss landscapes, a phenomenon often associated with the geometry of the minima found by stochastic optimization. We study how incremental grow-and-optimize strategies bias training toward flatter regions by viewing growth as progressive constraint relaxation. Starting from a low-dimensional submodel, we iteratively expand the trainable parameters by unlocking nested random subspaces while freezing the orthogonal complement at the network initialization, re-optimizing after each expansion until the full architecture is reached. Under standard local regularity conditions around non-degenerate minima, we prove that local sublevel sets are well approximated by ellipsoids and that basin accessibility under frozen constraints can be characterized by an explicit effective curvature in the frozen directions. This leads to an explanation of the bias: progressive growth increases the relative weight of wide basins and suppresses sharp ones through a volume effect induced by the frozen constraints. We empirically validate these predictions in controlled toy landscapes and in a realistic ResNet/CIFAR-100 setting and confirm that although progressive subspace growth reliably produces flatter solutions, curvature reductions do not universally translate into improved test performance, highlighting subtleties in the flatness-generalization connection. The code is available at https://github.com/p0lcAi/Across-the-Loss-Landscape.

</details>


### [64] [IAPO: Influence-Aware Policy Optimization for Credit Assignment in Multi-Turn Service Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24588)
*Bo Ren,Yirong Mao,Yi Yang,Wenhui Que*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出影响感知策略优化（IAPO），通过将每个 rollout 表示为类型化影响依赖图，利用用户和工具观察作为证据，将支持使用和失败使用的结构转化为路由权重，重新分配轨迹级优势，从而解决多轮交互中智能体的稀疏奖励信用分配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多轮交互的服务型智能体场景中，任务信息随时间逐步展开而非在初始提示中完全指定，最终奖励无法判断哪些动作真正促进了任务解决。现有方法依赖其他轨迹的比较证据或重新采样的延续，或需要单独构建的步骤级学习信号，缺乏对 rollout 内部信息与错误流动的利用。

Method: IAPO 将每个完整的 rollout 表示为类型化影响依赖图，图中的节点为可训练的智能体动作，用户和工具的观察作为边证据。IAPO 将支持使用（support-use）和失败使用（failed-use）结构转化为路由权重，用于重新分配同一个轨迹级的优势信号，从而实现细粒度的信用分配。

Result: 在 Qwen3-4B 和 Qwen3-8B 上，IAPO 在三个服务智能体基准（τ²-Bench、UserBench、AgentChangeBench）上均优于多轮强化学习基线；BFCL-v4 Multi-Turn 进一步表明这些增益不会损害多轮函数调用性能。

Conclusion: 这项工作推进了对多轮用户交互中信用分配问题的理解，为从稀疏结果反馈训练服务智能体提供了一种原则性的方法。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents increasingly solve long-horizon tasks through multi-turn interactions with users and external tools. In these settings, relevant task information often unfolds over time rather than being fully specified at the initial prompt. Service agents make this challenge especially concrete: users may clarify or revise their goals, while tool responses provide information needed for subsequent decisions. Thus, a final reward alone cannot indicate which actions contributed to resolving the task. Recent methods rely on comparative evidence from other trajectories or resampled continuations, or on separately constructed step-level learning signals, to refine credit. However, a completed rollout already records how information and errors flow between agent actions. We introduce Influence-Aware Policy Optimization (IAPO), which represents each rollout as a typed influence-dependency graph over trainable agent actions, with user and tool observations serving as evidence. IAPO converts support-use and failed-use structure into routing weights that redistribute the same trajectory-level advantage. Experiments with Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B demonstrate superior performance over multi-turn reinforcement learning (RL) baselines across three service-agent benchmarks: {τ^2}-Bench, UserBench, and AgentChangeBench. BFCL-v4 Multi-Turn further shows that these gains do not compromise multi-turn function-calling performance. This work advances the understanding of credit assignment in multi-turn user interactions and provides a principled approach to training service agents from sparse outcome feedback.

</details>


### [65] [Delayed Optimizer-State Transport Shapes Short-Horizon Training Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24593)
*Jinhui Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究自适应优化器（如AdamW）中的动量状态传输（moment-state transport）对短视界训练决策的影响。通过在提交的未来小批量序列上，差异化八步AdamW轨迹（通过完整的模型-优化器状态）并在独立评估前选择暴露匹配的Math-Code损失调度，发现完整的动量传输在12个未使用的0.3M Transformer历史中，10个历史相对于优化器感知的即时导数降低了token不相交损失（平均收益4.71e-4，p=0.0193）。两个控制器操作频率相同但60/96窗口中选择不同调度。交叉检查点-未来路径测试表明这种重排序源于优化器状态与近期未来数据的交互，独立Ising-CNN实验进一步证明删除动量状态传输会破坏准确的响应预测。结论是优化器记忆和近期未来数据顺序是训练状态的可操作组件，为有限视界而非单步干预提供了基于机制的标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应优化器在动量变量中保留梯度历史，使得损失加权的局部变化能够影响后续更新。作者试图探究这种延迟传输是否大到足以改变潜在短期（短视界）决策。如果优化器记忆确实影响近期决策路径，那么就需要考虑有限视界干预而非单步干预。

Method: 在提交的固定未来小批量序列上，通过完整的模型-优化器状态差异化八步AdamW轨迹。选择暴露匹配的Math-Code损失调度后进行独立评估。在12个未使用的0.3M Transformer训练历史上进行实验，采用token不相交损失作为评估指标，使用exact one-sided sign test进行统计检验。还进行了交叉检查点-未来路径测试来分离优化器状态与近期未来数据的影响，以及独立Ising-CNN实验来验证动量状态传输的必要性。

Result: 完整传输在10/12个历史中优于优化器感知的即时导数（平均收益4.71e-4，p=0.0193）。两个控制器操作频率相同但在60/96窗口中选择不同调度。交叉测试表明重排序源于优化器状态与近期未来数据的交互。Ising-CNN实验显示删除动量状态传输会破坏准确响应预测。完整传输分数在更大候选库中集中精确rollout胜者，便于聚焦有限幅度评估。

Conclusion: 在提交的短路径上，优化器记忆和近期未来数据顺序是训练状态中可操作（actionable）的组成部分。这意味着当优化器动量传输显著影响短期决策时，需要采用有限视界（finite-horizon）而非单步（one-step）干预策略。论文提供了一个基于机制的标准来判断何时需要哪种干预方式。

Abstract: Adaptive optimizers retain gradient history in moment variables, allowing a local change in loss weighting to alter later updates. We examine whether this delayed transport is large enough to change prospective short-horizon decisions. On committed future-minibatch sequences, we differentiate eight-step AdamW trajectories through the complete model--optimizer state and select exposure-matched Math--Code loss schedules before independent evaluation. Across 12 unused 0.3M Transformer histories, full transport lowers token-disjoint loss relative to an optimizer-aware immediate derivative in 10/12 histories (mean benefit $4.71\times10^{-4}$; exact one-sided sign test, $p=0.0193$). The two controllers act equally often but select different schedules in 60/96 windows. Crossed checkpoint--future-path tests attribute this reordering to the interaction between optimizer state and near-future data, while an independent Ising--CNN experiment shows that deleting moment-state transport destroys accurate response prediction. Full-transport scores also concentrate exact-rollout winners in larger candidate libraries, focusing finite-amplitude evaluation on a shortlist. On these committed short paths, optimizer memory and near-future data order are therefore actionable components of the training state, providing a mechanism-based criterion for when finite-horizon rather than one-step intervention is required.

</details>


### [66] [Taming foundation model with invariance-oriented pre-training for broad-spectrum EEG analysis across signal-level, brain-state, and brain-health tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24597)
*Yulong Dou,Han Wu,Guo Chen,Fangmao Ju,Zhiming Cui,Dinggang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: INCEPT是一个面向不变性的EEG基础模型，在超过11,000小时的未标注临床脑电数据上训练。不同于以重建为中心的传统EEG基础模型，INCEPT通过学习相关EEG观测之间的表征级稳定性来分离稳定的神经结构与受试者敏感信息，同时保留受试者、状态和条件判别信息。在涵盖信号级评估、脑状态解码和脑健康评估三个分析层级的十个数据集基准上，INCEPT在30个线性探针指标中排名第一于26个，在30个微调指标中排名第一于24个，并超越了任务特定的专家编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG模型大多遵循"一个数据集一个模型"的有监督范式，而最近的EEG基础模型多以重建为中心，假设可从局部上下文预测的EEG内容必然是可迁移的神经信息。然而头皮记录中包含大量干扰变异，重建目标可能无法有效捕捉真正可迁移的神经结构。因此需要一种面向不变性的预训练原则，以更好地构建可复用的EEG基础模型。

Method: INCEPT采用不变性导向的预训练策略：在超过11,000小时的未标注临床EEG数据上训练，核心思想是学习相关EEG观测之间的表征级稳定性，将稳定的神经结构和受试者敏感的关键信息与主导头皮记录的干扰变异分离开来，同时保持受试者、状态和条件判别信息。这与传统重建中心的方法形成对比。

Result: 在十个数据集、三个分析层级（信号级评估、脑状态解码、脑健康评估）的广谱基准上，INCEPT在30个线性探针指标中26个排名第一，在30个微调指标中24个排名第一，并在多种下游设置中超越了强任务特定的专家编码器。消融实验和表征分析表明，不变性导向的预训练比纯重建更能提升迁移性能并更好地组织受试者敏感的神经表征。

Conclusion: 不变性学习是构建可复用EEG基础模型的有效原则，能够超越重建中心的方法，为EEG基础模型的发展提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) is a widely used window into human brain function, but most EEG models remain tied to a one-dataset-one-model supervised paradigm. Recent EEG foundation models offer a route toward reusable representations, but most remain reconstruction-centered, assuming that EEG content predictable from local context is necessarily transferable neural information. Here we present INCEPT, an invariance-oriented EEG foundation model trained on over 11,000 hours of unlabelled clinical EEG. Rather than prioritizing signal recovery alone, INCEPT learns representation-level stability across correlated EEG observations, separating stable neural structure and essential subject-sensitive information from the nuisance variability that dominates scalp recordings while preserving subject-, state- and condition-discriminative information. We evaluate INCEPT on a broad-spectrum benchmark of ten datasets spanning three levels of post-acquisition EEG analysis: signal-level assessment, brain-state decoding, and brain-health evaluation. INCEPT ranks first among recent EEG foundation models on 26 of 30 linear-probing metrics and 24 of 30 fine-tuning metrics, and also surpasses strong task-specific specialist encoders across diverse downstream settings. Objective ablations and representation analyses further show that invariance-oriented pre-training improves transfer and organizes subject-sensitive neural representations beyond reconstruction alone. These results establish invariance learning as a promising principle for building reusable EEG foundation models.

</details>


### [67] [Bandit Submodular Maximization under Matroid Constraints: Learning Compressed Exchange Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24627)
*Zongqi Wan,Zhijie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了拟阵约束下单调子模函数的对抗性赌博机最大化问题，提出了首个在一般拟阵约束下具有亚线性遗憾的随机预言机多项式时间算法，其期望(1-1/e)-遗憾为Õ(n^{1/3}k^{2/3}T^{2/3})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗性赌博机设置下的子模函数最大化在一般拟阵约束下此前没有亚线性遗憾算法，这是一个长期未解决的理论挑战；同时需要在每次查询仅访问一个可行值的约束下实现高效学习。

Method: 将问题视为学习Poisson base walk的交换策略，将其转化为上下文赌博机问题以获取信息论意义上的亚线性遗憾保证；针对直接学习指数多个策略不可行的问题，引入"平衡分数交换"（balanced fractional exchanges），将策略混合压缩为单一分数基（fractional base），在保留Poisson分析所需交换信息的同时实现多项式时间复杂度。

Result: 得到了期望(1-1/e)-遗憾为Õ(n^{1/3}k^{2/3}T^{2/3})的随机预言机多项式时间算法，每个回合仅需一次可行值查询，这是首个在一般拟阵约束下具有亚线性遗憾的对抗性赌博机子模最大化算法。

Conclusion: 通过将对抗性赌博机子模最大化与上下文赌博机建立联系，并引入平衡分数交换的压缩技术，在保证亚线性遗憾的同时实现了多项式时间与空间复杂度，填补了该方向的理论空白。

Abstract: We study adversarial bandit maximization of monotone submodular functions under a matroid constraint. For a rank-$k$ matroid on $n$ elements, we give a randomized oracle-polynomial algorithm that makes one feasible value query per round and has expected $(1-1/e)$-regret $\widetilde O(n^{1/3}k^{2/3}T^{2/3})$. This is the first sublinear-regret algorithm for adversarial bandit submodular maximization under general matroid constraints.
  Technically, we view the problem as learning an exchange policy for the Poisson base walk. This connects the problem to contextual bandits and gives an information-theoretic sublinear-regret guarantee, but directly learning the exponentially many policies requires exponential time and space. We therefore introduce \emph{balanced fractional exchanges}, which compress the policy mixture into a single fractional base while retaining the exchange information needed by the Poisson analysis. This leads to an polynomial time algorithm with the same regret guarantee.

</details>


### [68] [Data Leakage Inflates Generalizability of Power Outage Prediction Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24665)
*Yamil Essus,Ranga Raju Vatsavai,Benjamin Rachunok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了停电预测模型在气候驱动基础设施风险评估中的泛化能力，发现常用随机划分评估方式因空间和时间自相关而高估模型性能；在更贴近实际部署的空间/时间/事件留出测试下，模型表现显著退化，甚至不优于零基线；GeoAI基础模型嵌入仅带来有限且不一致的改进，无法解决事件级迁移能力不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 停电预测模型日益用于气候驱动的风险评估，但当前评估实践掩盖了模型是否能在新情景（空间、时间、事件）下泛化的问题。作者旨在识别影响泛化的三个常见方法学选择，并量化不同评估策略对性能结论的扭曲程度。

Method: 使用美国东海岸2018-2023年公开数据，构建基于天气再分析、土地覆盖数据以及GeoAI基础模型（Prithvi WxC）嵌入的特征集，对比多种测试选择策略（未过滤随机划分、留一州、留一事件）下的预测性能差异。

Result: 随机划分下性能良好，但结果被空间和时间自相关显著膨胀；在空间/时间留出实验中性能大幅退化，模型常无法胜过简单零基线；GeoAI嵌入改进有限且不一致，仅对空间泛化略有帮助，无法解决事件级迁移差异。

Conclusion: 在现行数据可得性和评估实践下，公开训练的停电预测模型操作价值有限且不确定。未来需改善数据覆盖、采用更现实的评估协议，并将关注点从边际建模改进转向结构性数据约束的解决。

Abstract: Power outage prediction models are increasingly used in assessments of climate-driven infrastructure risk, yet current evaluation practices obscure whether these models generalize to the novel conditions such applications require. We identify three common methodological choices in power outage prediction models that influence their ability to generalize across spatial, temporal, and event-based settings. We compare the predictive performance impacts of different methodological decisions using publicly available data for the U.S. East Coast from 2018 to 2023 and feature sets derived from weather reanalysis and land-cover data, and embeddings from a GeoAI foundation model (Prithvi WxC). Specifically, we assess model performance under multiple test selection strategies, including unfiltered random splits, leave-one-state-out, and leave-one-event-out designs, which increasingly approximate real-world deployment conditions. While random train-test splits yield strong performance, we show that these results are inflated by spatial and temporal autocorrelation. Under spatial and temporal holdout experiments, predictive accuracy degrades substantially, with models often failing to outperform a simple null baseline. Incorporating GeoAI foundation model embeddings yields limited and inconsistent improvements, primarily for spatial generalization, and does not resolve poor event-level transferability. These findings suggest that, given current data availability and evaluation practices, publicly trained outage prediction models offer limited and uncertain operational value. Progress will likely require improved data coverage, more realistic evaluation protocols, and a shift in focus from marginal modeling advances toward addressing structural data constraints.

</details>


### [69] [A Multimodal Foundation Model for Longitudinal Patient Representation and Scalable Insight Generation in Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24688)
*Eugene Vorontsov,Yi Kan Wang,Alican Bozkurt,Adam Casson,Ludmila Tydlitatova,Michal Zelechowski,Ezra E. W. Cohen,Jyoti D. Patel,Max Banaszak,Caitlin McWilliams,Shane Colley,Kate Sasser,Ryan Fukushima,Eric Lefkofsky,Razik Yousfi,Siqi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了oFM，一个基于167万真实癌症患者队列开发的基础模型，整合临床轨迹与DNA、RNA和H&E病理数据，将每日临床和分子事件编码为患者状态嵌入。在预后基准测试中，oFM在治疗反应、无进展生存期和总生存期方面的AUC显著优于专家特征（总生存期0.774 vs 0.563），在11个治疗比较队列中实现三倍更高的治疗获益AUTOC，并通过证据基础的时序图实现机制发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准肿瘤学需要能够捕捉癌症演变和治疗过程的多模态纵向患者状态模型，但现有方法缺乏整合临床轨迹与分子病理数据的统一框架，难以对治疗反应和生存结局进行精确预测及机制解释。

Method: oFM在167万癌症患者上训练，以患者级别划分训练/验证/测试集（超过百万患者用于训练）。模型编码每日临床和分子事件序列，并结合H&E病理图像，随时间整合生成患者状态嵌入。评估时使用冻结的oFM嵌入与专家策划的临床和分子基线特征对比，并引入机制发现框架，通过证据基础的时序图解释下游模型预测。

Result: oFM在预后基准中显著提升AUC（治疗反应、PFS和OS，如OS从0.563提升至0.774）；在11个比较治疗队列中，oFM嵌入的合并和尺度归一化治疗获益AUTOC比基线高3倍，且在9/11队列中获益排序更优；两个治疗臂内均提供更强的预后区分能力。

Conclusion: oFM作为精准肿瘤学的多模态纵向基础模型，不仅能提升预后和治疗反应预测的准确性与治疗比较的获益估计，还通过证据基础的时序图机制发现框架，为临床决策和药物开发提供了可解释的、生物机制驱动的评估能力。

Abstract: Precision oncology necessitates a longitudinal model of patient state that captures cancer evolution and treatment over time, integrating multimodal observations. We introduce the oFM, a foundation model developed on a real-world oncology cohort of 1.67 million cancer patients that integrates clinical trajectories with DNA, RNA, and H&E pathology. Patient-level partitions were reserved for training, validation, and testing, with over one million patients used for training. The oFM encodes daily clinical and molecular episodes and, along with pathology images, integrates them over time to produce a patient state embedding. We evaluate frozen oFM embeddings against expert-curated clinical and molecular baseline features. In prognostic benchmarks, the oFM improved AUC for treatment response, progression-free survival, and overall survival (0.774 vs. 0.563 for overall survival). Across 11 comparative-treatment cohorts, the oFM embeddings achieved a three-fold higher pooled and scale-normalized treatment-benefit AUTOC than baseline features with improved benefit ranking in 9 of 11 cohorts, and provided stronger prognostic discrimination within both treatment arms. We also evaluated a mechanism discovery framework that interprets downstream models built on oFM embeddings by linking their predicted outcomes to clinically and biologically grounded mechanisms through an evidence-grounded temporal graph, enabling evaluation in clinical and drug-development applications.

</details>


### [70] [On-policy Distillation with Verifiable Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24696)
*Wenze Lin,Jiale Zhao,Xitai Jiang,Songde Rao,Yining Li,Shenzhi Wang,Bingxiang He,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OPDVR方法，无缝融合在线策略蒸馏（OPD）与可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR），无需引入额外超参数，通过在采样令牌蒸馏中加入基于轨迹正确性的ReLU门控机制，同时保留教师分布引导与任务级正确性信号，在六个推理基准上优于标准OPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR提供稀疏的任务级反馈，OPD提供密集的令牌级指导但忽略轨迹正确性且性能受限于教师模型。现有两者结合方式依赖加权组合或启发式切换，引入了额外超参数和权衡，缺乏简洁有效的统一方案。

Method: 提出OPDVR方法：首先基于轨迹正确性重新表述采样令牌OPD的隐式奖励；然后应用ReLU门控机制，确保正确轨迹获得非负奖励、错误轨迹获得非正奖励，从而在保留教师分布引导的同时对齐任务成功信号；该方法将采样令牌OPD转化为一个规范的RLVR方法，可与任意策略梯度算法（如GRPO）直接结合，无需添加任何超参数。

Result: 在六个推理基准上的实验表明，OPDVR始终优于标准OPD方法，验证了其有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: OPDVR通过简洁的ReLU门控机制将OPD与RLVR无缝统一，既弥补了OPD忽略轨迹正确性的缺陷，又解决了RLVR反馈稀疏的问题，且无需额外超参数调优，是一种实用且可扩展的LLM后训练方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) and on-policy distillation (OPD) have become two widely adopted paradigms for post-training large language models.
