However, RLVR suffers from sparse task-level feedback, while OPD provides dense token-level guidance but ignores trajectory correctness, limiting its performance to that of the teacher. Combining them is a promising direction: OPD supplies dense supervisory signals, while RLVR provides task-level correctness. Nevertheless, existing integrations often rely on weighted combination or heuristic switching, introducing extra hyperparameters and trade-offs. We propose On-policy Distillation with Verifiable Reward (OPDVR), a simple yet effective method that seamlessly combines OPD and RLVR without adding any hyperparameters. We first reformulate the implicit reward of sampled-token OPD based on trajectory correctness, then apply a ReLU gating mechanism to ensure that correct trajectories receive non-negative rewards and incorrect ones receive non-positive rewards---thereby aligning the distillation signal with task success while preserving the teacher's distributional guidance. Furthermore, our modification transforms sampled-token OPD into a proper RLVR method, making it readily combinable with any policy gradient algorithm, such as GRPO. Experiments on six reasoning benchmarks show that OPDVR consistently outperforms standard OPD. Our code is available at https://github.com/LeapLabTHU/OPDVR.

</details>


### [71] [Single State Update Predictive Coding training for Time Series Forecasting and Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24697)
*Matteo Cardoni,Sam Leroux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种将生成式预测编码网络（PCN）与支持编码PCN配对训练的并行训练技术，消除PCN中顺序反向误差传播的瓶颈，并将其应用于时间序列异常检测，实现更稳定、连续、在线的学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测编码（PC）是一个能够实现神经网络层并行更新的神经学习范式，但PCN的主要瓶颈在于顺序的反向误差传播（sequential backwards error propagation），这限制了其并行效率和训练速度。

Method: 引入一种训练技术，将一个生成式PCN与一个支持编码PCN配对。两个PCN并行训练以匹配彼此的神经激活状态，从而避免顺序传播。该方法被应用于时间序列异常检测任务。

Result: 实验表明该方法在时间序列异常检测上取得了更稳定、连续、在线学习的效果。

Conclusion: 通过并行配对两个PCN并匹配其神经激活，成功消除了PCN的顺序反向传播瓶颈，提升了时间序列异常检测中学习的稳定性和在线性。

Abstract: Predictive Coding (PC) is a neural learning paradigm that enables parallelizable neural network layer updates. However, the main bottleneck of PC Networks (PCN) is the sequential backwards error propagation. To tackle this, we introduce a training technique that pairs a Generative PCN with a support Encoding PCN. The two PCNs are trained in parallel to match their neural activations, without sequential propagation. We apply this to time series anomaly detection and show that our approach results in more stable, continuous, online learning.

</details>


### [72] [Parameter-Level Attribution of Symmetry in Trained Networks Though Parameter-Wise Functional Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24700)
*Alan Muriithi,Vedanta Thapar,Torben Berndt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了当网络已学习到具有已知对称性的函数时，能否将该对称性通过参数化移动——即参数空间中是否存在实现函数空间中群作用的运动。作者将问题形式化为实现映射Φ的提升问题，证明光滑参数空间作用存在当且仅当函数对称轨道的切空间位于微分dΦ_θ的像内（即参数的函数灵敏度张成的空间）。该条件也是逐点一阶提升的充分条件。用最小二乘松弛后，得到两个局部参数方向：一个沿对称轨道运动，另一个向等变子空间下降，残差度量参数化无法到达的部分。在旋转不变分类器和哈密顿神经网络上的实验验证了这些方向仅在局部诱导预期的函数空间运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解已训练网络的对称性是否能够"穿过"参数化，即能否通过参数空间中的连续运动在函数空间实现对称群作用，这在对称性理论、可解释性和模型分析中具有基础意义。作者将神经网络视为参数空间到函数空间的映射，从微分几何的视角提出提升问题，旨在建立参数层面实现函数对称性的充要条件。

Method: 将对称群的函数空间作用能否被参数化实现的问題形式化为提升问题（lifting problem）。首先导出一个必要且充分的条件：参数空间存在光滑群作用当且仅当对称轨道的切空间包含在dΦ_θ的像中，其中dΦ_θ的列向量即各参数的"功能灵敏度"。该条件对逐点一阶提升是充分的。随后通过最小二乘松弛该条件，推导出两个局部参数方向：一个沿对称轨道运动（由最小二乘投影给出），另一个向等变子空间下降。残差项度量参数化无法覆盖的对称方向。实验在旋转不变分类器和旋转对称势能的哈密顿神经网络上进行验证。

Result: 实验表明，求解得到的这两个参数方向确实在函数空间中诱导了预期的运动，但这仅在局部成立：重新计算的参数方向能够跟踪对称轨道并减小等变缺陷，而固定不变的方向在训练后偏离对称轨道和等变子空间。该结果在两类架构上均成立：一类是显式旋转不变分类器，另一类是不显式强制对称性的哈密顿神经网络。

Conclusion: 参数空间中实现函数对称性的运动仅在局部存在且有效，依赖于参数化对对称方向的覆盖能力（由功能灵敏度矩阵的列空间决定）。残差量化了参数化在对称方向上的不可达程度，为理解网络如何编码且是否能够实现对称性提供了新的微分几何框架。

Abstract: When a network has learned a function with a known symmetry, can that symmetry be moved through the parametrisation---is there a motion in parameter space realising the group action in function space? We formulate this as a lifting problem for the realisation map $Φ:θ\mapsto f_θ$, and show that a smooth parameter-space action exists only if the tangent space to the function's symmetry orbit lies within the image of $\mathrm dΦ_θ$, whose columns are the \emph{functional sensitivities} of individual parameters. This condition is also sufficient for pointwise first-order lifting. Relaxing it in least squares yields two local parameter directions: one following the symmetry orbit, one descending towards the equivariant subspace, with residuals measuring what the parametrisation cannot reach. On a rotationally invariant classifier we find these directions induce their predicted function-space motion, but only locally: recomputed directions track the orbit and reduce the equivariance defect, while directions held fixed depart from both after training. The same holds for Hamiltonian neural networks trained on a rotationally symmetric potential, even though the architecture does not explicitly enforce the symmetry.

</details>


### [73] [Constrained Hyperparameter Optimization for Streaming Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24712)
*Bruno Veloso,João Gama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对在线学习场景下超参数优化中的边界约束问题，提出了四种边界约束处理策略，并通过实验证明这些策略优于传统的"boundary"策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超参数优化对模型性能至关重要，但现有研究主要集中于批量学习场景，忽视了数据流中在线学习的复杂性。此外，许多超参数受限于有界搜索空间，传统的优化算子可能产生无效解，因此需要有效的边界约束处理技术来修正这些无效解。

Method: 本文提出了四种用于在线优化算法的边界约束处理策略，将超出边界阈值的超参数值重新对齐至相应限制。通过在公开数据集上进行实证研究，对比新策略与传统"boundary"策略的性能表现。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的四种边界策略在性能上均优于传统的"boundary"策略。

Conclusion: 在在线优化算法中，采用更精细的边界约束处理策略可以显著优于简单地将越界值直接截断到边界的传统方法，为在线学习场景下的超参数自适应优化提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Optimization of hyperparameters is a critical factor to obtain optimal model performance. While existing research has predominantly concentrated on batch-learning scenarios, addressing the complexities inherent in data streams presents a challenge. The deployment of sophisticated methodologies to manage data streams becomes highly important. Consequently, the capacity for self-adjusting hyperparameters during on-line learning phases emerges as a goal. Many hyperparameters exhibit constraints and are confined within bounded search spaces, rendering specific solutions unacceptable upon applying optimization operators. To solve this issue, employing boundary constraint- handling techniques becomes imperative to rectify invalid solutions. This paper presents strategies for effectively managing boundary constraints within constrained numerical optimization problems. Recent methodologies, including heuristic and evolutionary-based optimization, employ a "boundary" strategy, wherein values that surpass boundary thresholds for a given hyperparameter are realigned to the respective limits. Our study introduces four strategies to navigate boundary constraints in online optimization algorithms. Through empirical investigations conducted on established datasets, we demonstrate that adopting boundary strategies outperforms the "boundary" strategy.

</details>


### [74] [Enhancing Bayesian Optimization and Active Learning Through Kernel Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24721)
*Heng Zhang,Haotian Xiang,Qin Lu,Konstantinos D. Polyzos,Tara Javidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统一框架KENDO，将集成高斯过程（EGP）与分歧感知采集策略相结合，以替代贝叶斯优化和贝叶斯主动学习中昂贵的MCMC超参数采样，实现竞争性或更优性能的同时大幅降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化（BO）和贝叶斯主动学习（AL）中的超参数选择是关键挑战：模型误设会导致次优性能，而全贝叶斯处理虽然更准确但依赖计算昂贵的MCMC采样。

Method: 提出KENDO（Kernel ENsemble Disagreement-aware Operator）框架，核心思想是用核集成与自适应贝叶斯权重替代传统的超参数采样，并结合分歧感知采集策略。在该框架下分别实例化KENDO-BO（用于贝叶斯优化）和KENDO-AL（用于贝叶斯主动学习），两者共享共同的自校正机制但具有任务特定的采集目标。进一步通过随机标量化扩展至多目标优化，保持单优化器条件结构。

Result: 在合成及真实世界基准上的大量数值实验表明：（i）KENDO-BO在单目标优化、多目标优化中达到与最先进方法竞争或更优的性能，同时计算开销降低至多5倍；（ii）KENDO-AL在预测校准上优于基于MCMC的主动学习基线，提速至多27倍。

Conclusion: KENDO作为一个统一框架有效解决了BO和AL中超参数选择难题，通过核集成与分歧感知策略在保持性能的同时大幅降低计算成本，并为多目标优化提供了自然扩展，具有实用性和普适性。

Abstract: Hyperparameter selection remains a key challenge in Bayesian optimization (BO) and Bayesian active learning (AL), as model misspecification can lead to suboptimal performance, while more accurate fully Bayesian treatments typically rely on computationally expensive MCMC sampling. This paper proposes a unified framework, KENDO (Kernel ENsemble Disagreement-aware Operator), that integrates Ensemble Gaussian Processes (EGP) with disagreement-aware acquisition strategies. The central idea is to replace hyperparameter sampling with a kernel ensemble and adaptive Bayesian weighting, combined with disagreement-aware acquisition strategies. Within this unified framework, we instantiate KENDO-BO for BO and KENDO-AL for Bayesian AL, demonstrating that both arise from a common self-correcting mechanism with task-specific acquisition objectives. We further extend the approach to multi-objective optimization via random scalarization that preserves the single-optimizer conditioning structure. Thorough numerical tests on synthetic and real-world benchmarks across single-objective optimization, multi-objective optimization, and active learning demonstrate that (i) KENDO-BO achieves competitive or superior optimization performance compared to state-of-the-art methods while reducing computational overhead by up to $5\times$ and (ii) KENDO-AL achieves superior predictive calibration over MCMC-based active learning baselines with up to $27\times$ speedup.

</details>


### [75] [Parameter-Efficient Self-Supervised Adaptation for EEG-FM under Fixed Computational Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24727)
*Meghal Dani,Stefanie Liebe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了参数高效的自我监督自适应方法用于脑电图（EEG）基础模型的适配，仅更新9%的参数即可将预训练模型对齐到目标任务。在三个临床EEG数据集上验证了该方法优于线性探测，最高可提升20倍AUCPR，且仅需20-50%的未标注数据即可达到峰值性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型虽然通过自监督预训练获得了可迁移的表征，但其泛化能力在临床数据集中依然有限；同时，全量微调在资源受限的临床场景下因计算需求过高而不实用。因此需要探索参数高效的适配方法。

Method: 提出参数高效的自我监督适配方法，仅更新预训练模型中9%的参数来将表征对齐到目标任务。在两种不同预训练目标的先进模型（BIOT对比学习、CBraMod掩码重建）上，针对三个临床EEG数据集（TUAB异常检测、TUEV事件分类、CHB-MIT癫痫检测）的分布内和分布外条件下进行评估。

Result: 自监督适配相比线性探测带来一致的性能提升，最高提升20倍AUCPR；在固定计算预算下，仅需20-50%的可用未标注数据即可达到峰值性能；当总窗口数固定时，性能对患者数量不敏感，表明性能依赖于整体时间窗口多样性。

Conclusion: 参数高效的自我监督适配方法能够在极小的计算开销和数据收集负担下有效部署EEG基础模型，为资源受限的临床环境提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: EEG foundation models pretrained via self-supervised learning promise transferable representations, but their generalization remains limited, especially across diverse clinical datasets. Full fine-tuning is impractical for resource-constrained clinical settings due to high computational requirements. In this work, we investigate whether parameter-efficient self-supervised adaptation, updating only 9% of parameters suffices to align representations to target tasks. We evaluate our method on two state-of-the-art models with different pretraining objectives: BIOT (contrastive) and CBraMod (masked reconstruction), and evaluate on three clinical EEG datasets for abnormality detection (TUAB), event classification (TUEV), and seizure detection (CHB-MIT) under both in-distribution and out-of-distribution conditions. SSL adaptation yields consistent gains over linear probing, up to 20x AUCPR. Under a fixed compute budget, peak performance requires only 20--50% of available unlabeled data. Critically, when total window count is fixed, performance remains invariant to patient count, suggesting that performance is dependent on overall temporal window diversity only. Our findings demonstrate that parameter-efficient adaptation enables effective deployment of EEG Foundation models (EEG-FM) with minimal computational overhead and data collection burden. Code available at: https://github.com/c3n-group/efficient-eeg-adapt

</details>


### [76] [Optimal Alternating Regret for Online Learning and Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24731)
*Yixin Tao,Weiqiang Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在在线线性优化（OLO）和在线凸优化（OCO）中解决了极小极大最优的交替遗憾问题。对于概率单纯形上的OLO，给出了常数交替遗憾的算法（O(log d)），并证明匹配下界；对于一般OCO，给出了O(d log(1+T/d))的交替遗憾算法，同样证明匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替遗憾是博弈论中由交替学习动力学所驱动的遗憾概念，此前仅有次优结果（如O(log^{2/3}d·T^{1/3})），需要建立极小极大最优的交替遗憾界限。同时，更优的交替遗憾直接意味着博弈中更快的均衡收敛速率。

Method: 对于OLO在单纯形上的情况，提出一种能达到常数交替遗憾的算法（不依赖时间T）；对于一般OCO，提出达到O(d log(1+T/d))交替遗憾的算法。两者均通过构造匹配的上下界来证明极小极大最优性。

Result: 1) OLO/单纯形：O(log d)常数交替遗憾（与T无关），匹配下界；由此得到二人零和博弈中O(log d/T)收敛到Nash均衡，二人一般和博弈中O(log d/T)收敛到coarse correlated equilibrium（CCE），这是首个O(1/T)收敛到CCE的非耦合学习动力学。2) 一般OCO：O(d log(1+T/d))交替遗憾，匹配下界Ω(d log(1+T/d))，表明Ω(log T)因子不可消除。

Conclusion: 本文为交替遗憾在OLO和OCO两种设定下都建立了完整的极小极大最优刻画，同时展示了更优的交替遗憾在博弈均衡收敛（尤其是O(1/T)收敛到CCE）中的直接收益。

Abstract: We settle the minimax-optimal alternating regret, a regret notion motivated by alternating learning dynamics in games, for both online linear optimization (OLO) and online convex optimization (OCO).
  For OLO over the probability simplex $Δ_d$, we give an algorithm with $O(\log d)$ alternating regret that remains a constant for any time horizon $T$, and a matching lower bound. Our constant regret bound significantly improves previous results with $O(\log ^{2/3}d \cdot T^{1/3})$ regret [Cevher, Cutkosky, Kavis, Piliouras, Skoulakis, Viano, NeurIPS 2023, Hait, Li, Luo, Zhang, COLT 2025]. As a result, we obtain alternating learning dynamics with $O(\log d /T)$ convergence to Nash equilibria in two-player zero-sum games and $O(\log d /T)$ convergence to coarse correlated equilibria in two-player general-sum games. This is the first uncoupled learning dynamics with $O(1/T)$ convergence to CCE in two-player general-sum games, while all prior works suffer additional $\log T$ factors.
  For general OCO over a $d$-dimensional compact convex set, we give an algorithm with $O(d\log (1+T/d))$ alternating regret, improving the previous best of $\widetilde{O}(d^{2/3}T^{1/3})$. We also prove a matching lower bound of $Ω(d\log (1+T/d))$, showing that the $Ω(\log T)$ factor is unavoidable.

</details>


### [77] [$(\text{DNN})^2$: Doubly Non-Negative Relaxations for Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24743)
*Hanna Jiamei Zhang,Alan Papalia,Michael Everett,David M. Rosen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出(DNN)²方法，通过Burer-Monteiro分解结合特征值最大化过程搜索非唯一对偶乘子空间，为ReLU神经网络验证中的双非负规划(DNN)松弛提供全局最优性证书，产生比标准SDP方法更紧的边界且常能匹配精确解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReLU神经网络验证中，LP和SDP松弛存在显著松弛间隙导致过于保守的安全保证。CPP公式能消除间隙但NP难解。DNN是CPP最便宜的可行松弛，保留关键约束为SDP，但其规模超出内点法的实际可达范围。虽然Burer-Monteiro分解已用于使SDP验证可扩展，但DNN中额外的非负约束导致对偶乘子非唯一，使得标准认证方法失效，因此尚无DNN的可扩展解法。

Method: 提出一种新的特征值最大化过程，在DNN松弛中非唯一的对偶乘子空间中搜索有效证书（即全局最优性保证）。该方法利用Burer-Monteiro分解实现DNN的可扩展求解，并通过搜索对偶乘子空间来克服标准认证方法因乘子非唯一而失效的问题，从而为DNN的最优解提供可验证的全局最优性证明。

Result: 实验表明(DNN)²方法产生的边界一致地比标准SDP方法更紧，且常常能匹配精确解。同时，认证过程能在存在有效证书时确认全局最优性。

Conclusion: 该工作是向提供紧致、可认证且计算可扩展的神经网络验证保证迈出的关键一步，有望支持将神经网络控制器和感知模块部署于安全关键的自主系统。

Abstract: Existing linear program (LP) and semidefinite program (SDP) relaxations for rectified linear unit (ReLU) neural network (NN) verification yield overly-conservative safety guarantees due to significant relaxation gaps. While the completely positive program (CPP) formulation closes this gap, it is NP-hard to solve. Its cheapest tractable relaxation, the doubly non-negative program (DNN), retains critical constraints as an SDP, but one whose size exceeds the reach of interior-point methods at practical scale. While Burer-Monteiro (BM) factorization has been applied to make SDP-based verification scalable, no such result exists for the strictly tighter DNN formulation. A key obstacle is that additional non-negativity constraints in the DNN cause dual multipliers for optimality certification to be non-unique, making standard certification methods inapplicable. We propose a novel eigenvalue maximization procedure that searches the non-unique multiplier space for a valid certificate, i.e. a global optimality guarantee. Experiments demonstrate that our approach $(\text{DNN})^2$ produces bounds consistently tighter than the standard SDP method, often matching the exact solution, and that our certification procedure confirms global optimality when a valid certificate exists. These results are a key step toward providing tight, certifiable, and computationally scalable verification guarantees needed to deploy neural network controllers and perception modules in safety-critical autonomous systems.

</details>


### [78] [Beyond Uniform Local Isometry and Topology: FactoMap for Disentangled Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24762)
*Sohini Gupta,Bahareh Tolooshams*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出因子空间结构（factor-space structure）概念，用于描述生成因子的几何特征（环绕、坍缩、位置相关尺度），并基于此提出Factor-Space Topographic Map（FactoMap）方法，通过因子空间晶格索引的可解释原型进行拓扑学习，在学习到的表示中传递晶格的周期性、坍缩和非均匀范围，从而实现因子连续性和解缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解缠方法将生成因子表示为欧几里得乘积坐标，但底层因子空间可能存在环绕（wrapping）、坍缩（collapse）或位置相关的几何结构。这些几何差异无法被欧几里得坐标所捕获，且统计独立的因子未必几何可分离（如色相与尺度产生不同增长速率的效果，各向异性无法通过固定重缩放消除）。因此需要一种能建模因子空间真实几何的解缠方法。

Method: 首先引入因子空间结构，将因子域、生成器诱导的同一性（identifications）和位置相关尺度结合起来，以区分拓扑等价但几何不同的空间。然后提出 FactoMap，通过因子空间晶格索引来学习可解释的原型，利用拓扑学习将晶格的周期性、坍缩和非均匀范围传递到学习到的表示中。

Result: 实验表明，匹配因子空间结构能够保持因子连续性，并实现对底层因子的解缠。

Conclusion: 因子空间的几何结构（而非仅仅拓扑结构）对于解缠至关重要；通过显式建模因子空间结构并利用拓扑图进行学习，可以在表示中保留因子的连续性并实现有效的解缠。

Abstract: Many disentanglement methods represent generative factors using Euclidean product coordinates, although the underlying factor spaces may wrap, collapse, or have position-dependent geometry. We introduce factor-space structure, combining factor domains, generator-induced identifications, and position-dependent scales to distinguish topologically equivalent spaces with different factor geometries. We show that statistically independent factors need not be geometrically separable: hue and scale produce effects that grow at different rates, yielding anisotropy that no fixed rescaling removes. We propose the Factor-Space Topographic Map (FactoMap), which learns interpretable prototypes indexed by a factor-space lattice. Topographic learning transfers the lattice's periodicity, collapses, and non-uniform extent to the representation. Experiments show that matching this structure preserves factor continuity and enables disentanglement of the underlying factors.

</details>


### [79] [LION: A Clifford Neural Paradigm for Multimodal-Attributed Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24795)
*Xunkai Li,Zekai Chen,Zhengyu Wu,Henan Sun,Daohan Su,Guang Zeng,Hongchao Qin,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LION（Clifford神经范式），基于Clifford代数和"传播-聚合"解耦图范式，在多模态属性图上实现"对齐-融合"。通过Clifford代数构建模态感知几何流形实现高次图传播以完成模态对齐，并提出自适应全息聚合模块实现模态融合，在9个文本-图像多模态属性图数据集上超越现有SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态领域的快速发展推动图机器学习从文本属性图转向多模态属性图，但现有神经范式存在两大局限：(1) 模态对齐阶段，多数方法采用拓扑约束或模态特化的tokenizer，忽视了图上下文并抑制了模态交互；(2) 模态融合阶段，多数方法仅针对双模态图进行简单适配，未能充分利用带有拓扑先验的对齐token，导致泛化性差和性能下降。

Method: 提出LION范式，包含两个核心模块：(1) 基于Clifford代数构建模态感知几何流形，通过几何诱导的高阶图传播高效实现模态交互与对齐；(2) 提出自适应全息聚合模块，利用对齐token的拓扑感知Clifford分量，结合分量能量和传播尺度信息及可学习参数，实现自适应模态融合。整体采用"传播-聚合"解耦范式实现"对齐-融合"两阶段流程。

Result: 在9个文本-图像多模态属性图数据集上，LION在3个图和3个模态下游任务中均显著优于现有SOTA基线方法。

Conclusion: LION通过Clifford代数提供的几何归纳偏置和自适应全息聚合机制，有效解决了现有方法在模态对齐中忽视图上下文以及模态融合中泛化性不足的问题，为多模态属性图学习提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Recently, the rapid advancement of multimodal domains has driven a data-centric paradigm shift in graph ML, transitioning from text-attributed to multimodal-attributed graphs. This advancement significantly enhances data representation and expands the scope of graph downstream tasks, such as modality-oriented tasks, thereby improving the practical utility of graph ML. Despite its promise, limitations exist in the current neural paradigms:(1) Neglect Context in Modality Alignment: Most existing methods adopt topology-constrained or modality-specific operators as tokenizers.These aligners inevitably neglect graph context and inhibit modality interaction, resulting in suboptimal alignment.(2) Lack of Adaptation in Modality Fusion: Most existing methods are simple adaptations for 2-modality graphs and fail to adequately exploit aligned tokens equipped with topology priors during fusion, leading to poor generalizability and performance degradation.To address the above issues, we propose LION (c\underline{LI}ff\underline{O}rd \underline{N}eural paradigm) based on the Clifford algebra and decoupled graph neural paradigm (i.e., propagation-then-aggregation) to implement alignment-then-fusion in multimodal-attributed graphs. Specifically, we first construct a modality-aware geometric manifold grounded in Clifford algebra.This geometric-induced high-order graph propagation efficiently achieves modality interaction, facilitating modality alignment.Then, based on the topology-aware Clifford components of aligned tokens, we propose adaptive holographic aggregation. This module integrates component-wise energy and propagation-scale information with learnable parameters to improve modality fusion. Extensive experiments on 9 text-image MAG datasets demonstrate that LION significantly outperforms SOTA baselines across 3 graph and 3 modality downstream tasks.

</details>


### [80] [MDTE: Minority-Aware Diffusion over Temporal Edge Events for Imbalanced Node Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24812)
*Zhou Zelong,Zhang Tianming,Yang Zhengyi,Tang Yifu,Hou Chenyu,Cao Bin,Fan Jing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MDTE框架，一种面向时间图上的类别不均衡节点分类问题的少数类感知扩散框架。通过条件扩散去噪重建稳定且可区分的时序边事件表示，并引入分布感知选择性传播（结合LOF传播过滤与聚类感知低频传播）和多视图判别融合模块，有效缓解多数类对少数类的传播同化问题。在五个真实数据集上，MDTE在少数类指标上全面超越最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间图上的类别不均衡节点分类面临双重挑战：一方面，多数类主导的时间传播会渐进地同化少数类表示，导致少数类信息被淹没；另一方面，传统的节点和邻域信息无法为少数类提供足够的判别证据，使得少数类分类性能严重退化。

Method: MDTE包含两个核心模块：（1）Distribution-Aware Selective Propagation（分布感知选择性传播），采用LOF（局部异常因子）传播过滤与聚类感知低频传播相结合，保留信息丰富的邻域依赖关系的同时抑制有害传播和多数类信息同化；（2）Multi-View Discriminative Fusion（多视图判别融合），通过特征重建和拓扑预测刻画各类别在分布学习上的差异，提取互补的判别性信号以指导条件扩散去噪过程。

Result: 在五个真实数据集上的实验表明，MDTE在少数类导向的指标上持续取得最优性能：少数类召回率最高提升23.53个百分点，少数类F1最高提升8.68个百分点，AUPRC最高提升2.67个百分点，均相对最强基线。

Conclusion: MDTE通过将少数类感知的传播机制与条件扩散去噪相结合，有效解决了时间图上类别不均衡导致的少数类表征同化问题，为少数类节点分类提供了稳定且可判别的表示学习方案。

Abstract: Class-imbalanced node classification on temporal graphs is challenging because majority-dominated temporal propagation progressively assimilates minority representations, while conventional node and neighborhood information provides insufficient discriminative evidence for minority classes. To address these issues, we propose MDTE, a minority-aware diffusion framework that reconstructs stable and discriminative temporal edge-event representations through conditional diffusion denoising. Specifically, MDTE introduces Distribution-Aware Selective Propagation, which combines Local Outlier Factor (LOF)-based propagation filtering with cluster-aware low-frequency propagation. The module preserves informative neighborhood dependencies while mitigating harmful propagation and majority-class information assimilation. It further develops Multi-View Discriminative Fusion, which exploits feature reconstruction and topology prediction to characterize class-wise differences in distribution learning and extracts complementary discriminability signals to guide denoising. Experiments on five real-world datasets demonstrate that MDTE consistently achieves the best performance on minority-class-oriented metrics, improving minority-class recall by up to 23.53 percentage points, minority-class F1 by 8.68 percentage points, and AUPRC by 2.67 percentage points over the strongest baselines.

</details>


### [81] [Effective Learning Rate Governs Loss Dynamics in Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24814)
*Zihan Liu,Ruiheng Zheng,Shaobo Zhang,Changxin Tian,Kunlong Chen,Zhiqiang Zhang,Lei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现了语言模型预训练中的"有效学习率(ELR)坍缩"现象：损失动态主要由学习率与参数范数的比值（即有效学习率ELR）决定，而非二者单独决定。当ELR在不同运行间匹配时，即使LR和参数范数差异显著，损失轨迹在整个训练过程中也会高度重合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言模型预训练中损失动态的底层控制因素是提高可预测性和可控性的关键。以往研究分别考虑学习率和参数范数/归一化对训练的影响，但缺乏一个统一视角来解释二者如何共同塑造损失轨迹。作者旨在寻找一个"公共坐标"来统一描述LR调度、范数控制与损失动态之间的关系。

Method: 作者系统性地跨优化器、架构、数据集和模型规模进行对照实验，通过匹配ELR将各运行的损失轨迹进行对比并量化坍缩误差。通过系统消融实验识别归一化设计和LR-范数变化的时间尺度对坍缩精度的影响。此外，通过受控干预实验（如改变权重衰减和Hyperball）揭示它们主要通过诱导ELR调度来影响损失动态。最后将LR替换为ELR，检验拟合的函数缩放律(FSL)能否跨范数控制方法迁移。

Result: 跨各配置的均值坍缩误差通常为几×10⁻³量级，低于代表性配置中种子间变异度。系统性消融表明归一化设计和LR-范数变化时间尺度是坍缩精度的关键决定因素。受控干预证明权重衰减和Hyperball主要通过它们诱导的ELR调度影响损失动态。将LR替换为ELR后，拟合的函数缩放律(FSL)成功跨范数控制方法迁移，并能解释延迟加速现象。

Conclusion: ELR坍缩现象将LR、范数控制和损失动态统一在一个公共坐标下，ELR是连接三者关系的核心量。这一发现为语言模型预训练的可预测扩展和规范控制提供了新的理论视角和实践工具。

Abstract: We uncover ELR collapse in language model pretraining: learning rate (LR) and parameter norm govern loss dynamics primarily through their ratio, the effective learning rate (ELR). When ELR is matched across runs, their loss trajectories collapse throughout training despite substantially different LRs and parameter norms. Across optimizers, architectures, datasets, and model scales, mean collapse errors are typically a few x 10^-3, below the seed-to-seed variation measured in a representative configuration. Systematic ablations identify normalization design and the timescale of LR-norm variation as key determinants of collapse precision. Controlled interventions further show that weight decay and Hyperball shape loss dynamics primarily through the ELR schedules they induce. Replacing LR with ELR enables a fitted functional scaling law (FSL) to transfer across norm-control methods. The resulting ELR-based FSL also explains delayed acceleration, a recurring effect of norm control. Together, these results establish ELR as a common coordinate linking LR scheduling, norm control, and loss dynamics.

</details>


### [82] [A Geometric Theory of Robust Fairness Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24818)
*Binita Maity*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个几何框架来分析基于邻域的公平性审计在有限扰动下的鲁棒性，建立了邻域不变的充分条件，量化了邻域替换如何传导至审计不稳定性，并引入审计波动率作为反复扰动下公平性审计期望敏感性的度量。实验表明该框架能解释真实场景中邻域公平性审计的稳定性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于邻域的公平性审计通过比较特征空间中相似个体的预测来评估个体公平性，被广泛使用，但审计过程本身的鲁棒性鲜有研究。由于这类审计依赖最近邻关系，特征空间的微小扰动会改变局部邻域，即使在模型预测不变的情况下也可能产生不同的公平性评估结果。

Method: 作者开发了一个几何框架来分析有界扰动下邻域公平性审计的鲁棒性。具体包括：(1) 建立邻域不变的充分条件；(2) 量化邻域替换如何传导为审计不稳定性的累积过程；(3) 引入审计波动率（audit volatility），作为重复扰动下公平性审计期望敏感性的度量。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验支持理论分析，表明所提出的框架能够解释基于邻域的公平性审计在实际观测中的稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次从几何角度系统性地刻画了邻域公平性审计的鲁棒性，为审计方法的可靠性评估提供了理论工具，并给出了可操作的条件来理解何时邻域审计结果值得信懒。

Abstract: Neighborhood-based fairness audits evaluate individual fairness by comparing predictions among similar individuals in feature space. Despite their widespread use, little is known about the robustness of the auditing procedure itself. Because these audits rely on nearest neighbor relationships, small perturbations in feature space can alter local neighborhoods and produce different fairness assessments even when model predictions remain unchanged. We develop a geometric framework for analyzing the robustness of neighborhood-based fairness audits under bounded perturbations. Our analysis establishes sufficient conditions for neighborhood invariance, quantifies how neighborhood replacement propagates to audit instability, and introduces audit volatility, a measure of the expected sensitivity of fairness audits under repeated perturbations. Experiments on benchmark datasets support the theoretical analysis and show that the proposed framework explains the observed stability of neighborhood-based fairness audits.

</details>


### [83] [BioKERN: Biological Kernel Regularization for Histology-to-Transcriptomics Neighborhood Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24823)
*Seungik Cho,Betul Orcan-Ekmekci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BioKERN是一个多模态空间表征学习框架，通过将转录组相似性与空间邻近性结合构建显式的生物学核，在训练时提供分级邻域监督并正则化嵌入几何，从而在保留生物学邻域结构方面优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的组织学-转录组学多模态学习方法过度强调实例级精确匹配（instance-level matching），即使非配对位点（non-paired spots）具有共享的分子或空间上下文，也无法充分保留生物学邻域结构。作者认为空间生物学需要能够保留生物学邻域结构的表征，而不仅仅是跨模态的精确对应关系。

Method: BioKERN构建一个训练时的生物学核（biological kernel），该核结合了转录组相似性和空间邻近性，以此提供分级邻域监督（graded neighborhood supervision）并正则化嵌入几何（regularize embedding geometry），将生物学结构作为显式、可学习的归纳偏置（inductive bias）融入多模态学习框架。

Result: 在Mouse Brain Visium和Human Liver GSE240429两个数据集上，BioKERN在生物邻域检索（biological-neighborhood retrieval）任务中一致性地优于BLEEP方法，涵盖单尺度和多尺度设置。受控的共享架构实验表明，改进主要来自生物学核正则化而非模型容量的增加。

Conclusion: 显式的生物学几何作为可解释的归纳偏置，能有效提升多模态学习在空间生物学中的表现，支持将生物学结构知识直接编码到学习框架中的设计理念。

Abstract: Spatially resolved biology requires representations that preserve biological neighborhood structure rather than only exact cross-modal correspondences. Existing histology--transcriptomics objectives can emphasize instance-level matching even when non-paired spots share molecular or spatial context. We introduce BioKERN, a multimodal spatial representation-learning framework that incorporates biological structure as an explicit, learnable inductive bias. BioKERN constructs a training-time biological kernel by combining transcriptomic similarity and spatial proximity, then uses it to provide graded neighborhood supervision and regularize embedding geometry. Evaluation uses a fixed, model-independent biological neighborhood definition shared by all methods. Across Mouse Brain Visium and Human Liver GSE240429, BioKERN consistently improves biological-neighborhood retrieval over BLEEP in both single- and multi-scale settings. Controlled shared-architecture experiments show that most of the improvement arises from biological-kernel regularization rather than increased model capacity. These results support explicit biological geometry as an interpretable inductive bias for multimodal learning in spatial biology.

</details>


### [84] [Bellman Calibration for Marginalized Importance Weighting in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24858)
*Lars van der Laan,Nathan Kallus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出等渗Bellman校准（Isotonic Bellman Calibration），一种一维、模型无关的后处理方法，通过非递减变换类上的拟合占据率评估（FORE）来校正边际重要性加权估计器中的占据平衡违例，同时保留初始估计的排序信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的极小极大、原始对偶和拟合定点估计器因函数类逼近、正则化或优化不完整而会留下残差占据平衡违例。这些违例难以诊断和降低，因为目标函数通常缺乏直接的监督验证损失用于超参数调优、模型选择和早停。

Method: 方法上，等渗Bellman校准通过在非递减变换的一维函数类上应用拟合占据率评估（FORE）后处理方法，校正初始占据比率估计的尺度与形状。该方法被刻画为一个条件定点性质，等价于对校准比率的每个测试函数均满足占据平衡。理论上还推导了校准-细化界，并给出了有限样本校准保证和相对于最优单调变换的KL oracle不等式。

Result: 等渗Bellman校准在小校准误差和KL风险方面达到最佳单调校正的统计误差范围内，并能为下游目标占据泛函（包括策略价值估计）提供保证。

Conclusion: 等渗Bellman校准为离线策略评估中的占据平衡违例提供了可诊断、可验证的一维后处理解决方案，兼具理论保证与实用价值。

Abstract: Marginalized importance weighting evaluates a target policy by reweighting offline state-action samples with its discounted occupancy ratio, characterized by an adjoint Bellman equation. Existing minimax, primal-dual, and fitted fixed-point estimators can leave residual occupancy-balance violations because of function-class approximation, regularization, or incomplete optimization. These violations are difficult to diagnose and reduce because the objectives generally lack a direct supervised validation loss for hyperparameter tuning, model selection, and early stopping. We introduce isotonic Bellman calibration, a one-dimensional, model-agnostic post-processing method that reduces these violations while preserving the ranking information in any initial occupancy-ratio estimate. The method corrects the estimate's scale and shape by applying fitted occupancy-ratio evaluation (FORE) over a one-dimensional class of nondecreasing transformations. We characterize Bellman calibration as a conditional fixed-point property equivalent to occupancy-balance against every test function of the calibrated ratio. More generally, we derive a calibration-refinement bound showing that any fitted ratio with small calibration error performs nearly as well as the best post-processing based on its fitted values. For isotonic Bellman calibration, we establish finite-sample calibration guarantees and a KL oracle inequality relative to the best monotone transformation of the initial estimate. Consequently, isotonic Bellman calibration achieves small calibration error and KL risk within statistical error of the best monotone correction, with guarantees for downstream target-occupancy functionals, including policy-value estimation.

</details>


### [85] [Improving Cross-Problem Vehicle Routing with Locally Augmented Preferences and Representation Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24859)
*Arthur Corrêa,Paulo Nascimento,Samuel Moniz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多任务车辆路径规划（VRP）求解器提出两项模型无关的贡献：POLAR训练算法（通过局部搜索精化最优解码路径后再构建偏好对，显著提升pairwise margin信息量）和PLE渐进分层提取编码器（通过门控机制将共享专家与任务特定专家结合，逐步分离通用路由结构与约束特定编码）。两者结合在16个分布内变体上将平均gap降低21.3%，并在32个未见变体中的27个上超越先前神经方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经多任务VRP求解器存在两大局限：训练方面，强化学习受奖励尺度差异和优势信号衰减困扰，偏好优化则因采样路径趋于一致而受限于策略自身生成解的质量，导致两者后期监督信号薄弱；架构方面，全共享编码器将异构变体的约束相关表征纠缠在一起，限制泛化能力。

Method: 1) POLAR（Preference Optimization with Locally Augmented Refinement）：对最优解码路径执行局部搜索精化，再基于精化后的路径构建偏好对，生成信息量更丰富的偏好边界。2) PLE（Progressive Layered Extraction）编码器：每一编码器层通过门控机制路由至一个共享专家和一组任务特定专家，逐层渐进地分离通用路由结构与约束特定编码。两个组件均为模型无关的即插即用模块。

Result: 在16个分布内VRP变体上，相对最强发布基线将平均gap降低21.3%；在32个未见变体上，27个超越先前神经方法。消融实验验证了两个组分的有效性，且两者在多骨干模型架构上均提升跨问题泛化能力。

Conclusion: POLAR与PLE分别从训练信号强度和编码器表征解耦两个维度解决了多任务VRP求解器的核心瓶颈，二者协同可显著提升当前神经多任务求解器的SOTA水平，并具有跨架构的通用适用性。

Abstract: Multi-task vehicle routing problem (VRP) solvers seek to handle multiple VRP variants within a single unified model, avoiding the need to train a separate model for every variant. In spite of recent progress, current approaches remain limited on two fronts. On the training side, reinforcement learning suffers from reward-scale disparities and shrinking advantage signals as policies improve, whereas preference optimization stagnates once sampled tours become near-identical and thus fundamentally limited by the quality of the policy's own generated solutions, leaving both paradigms with weak supervision as training progresses. On the architecture side, existing fully shared encoders entangle constraint-dependent representations across heterogeneous variants, which limits generalization. We address these gaps with two model-agnostic contributions. First, we propose Preference Optimization with Locally Augmented Refinement (POLAR), a novel training algorithm that applies a local search refinement pass to the best decoded tour before forming preference pairs, yielding much more informative pairwise margins. Second, a Progressive Layered Extraction (PLE) encoder routes each encoder layer through one shared expert and a set of task-specific experts via a gating mechanism, progressively separating common routing structure from constraint-specific encodings. Through extensive experiments on various VRP variants, we show that POLAR and PLE together elevate the current state-of-the-art among neural multi-task solvers. We reduce the average gap to reference solutions by 21.3% relative to the strongest published baseline on 16 in-distribution variants, and outperform prior neural methods on 27 out of 32 unseen variants. Ablation studies confirm the efficacy of each contribution, showing that both improve cross-problem generalization across multiple backbone model architectures.

</details>

### [86] [A Survey of Timing Variability in Microservice-Based Software-Defined Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23649)
*Cyrus K. Vattes,Habib Mostafaei,Nirvana Meratnia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文综述了微服务系统中时序变异性的来源、传播机制和可观测影响，并以软件定义车辆为案例，比较了各类时序模型在工作负载稳定性、执行结构、资源共享、可观测性和保障可接受性方面的假设与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务系统虽然模块化且适应性强，但分布式结构使得其时序行为难以分析和保障。局部时序扰动可能通过网络依赖、共享资源和中间件传播为系统级效应，传统时序分析方法难以适用于此类环境。

Method: 采用综述方法：以软件定义车辆作为动机性案例，系统梳理时序变异性的来源（服务依赖、共享资源、协调中间件）与传播机制，并对主要时序模型类别按其假设维度（工作负载稳定性、执行结构、资源共享、可观测性、保障可接受性）进行比较分析。

Result: 总结了各类时序模型在微服务软件定义车辆场景下的局限，揭示了不同模型类别的适用边界及其与微服务实际运行条件之间的差距。

Conclusion: 现有时序模型无法完全覆盖微服务系统特有的时序变异性特征，需要发展新的分析框架来应对软件定义车辆等安全关键领域的时序保障需求。

Abstract: Microservice-based systems are modular and adaptable, but their distributed structure makes their timing behavior difficult to analyze and guarantee. Because latency emerges from interactions among service dependencies, shared resources, and coordinating middleware, local timing disturbances can propagate into system-level effects. This survey examines the sources, propagation mechanisms, and observable impacts of timing variability in microservice-based systems, using software-defined vehicles as a motivating example. It compares major classes of timing models by their assumptions about workload stability, execution structure, resource sharing, observability, and guarantee admissibility, and summarizes their limitations within microservice-based software-defined vehicles.

</details>


### [87] [Memory-Sovereign Inference: Output-Exact Execution Beyond Full Residency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23805)
*Lukas Stepanek*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可证伪的证书（falsifiable certificate）来区分存储推理中的表示、语义需求、调度请求与流量，并通过Qwen3-Next模型的主实验证明：LRU64执行满足契约，但stock路由器在32K prefill期间实际选择了全部48个层专家对象，其规范并集的语义需求下界（43.59 GiB）超出声明的34 GiB全驻留包络和11 GiB+24 GiB的host/device包络；异步完整组件在墙钟时间上仅为阻塞直接路径的32.3%，且64个输出词元的全部结果精确匹配零缓存oracle。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 存储后端推理容易被过度宣称：进程RSS排除计费页面缓存，进程本地设备读数排除板级使用，而成功的生成并不能证明异步复用的正确性。作者希望提供一个可证伪的框架，明确区分各层资源语义，防止对内存驻留、响应速度及异步拷贝行为的夸大或不实声明。

Method: 提出一种可证伪证书，涵盖四个分离维度：表示（representation）、语义需求（semantic demand）、调度请求（scheduler requests）与流量（traffic），并明确资源权威（resource authorities）、精确性视界（exactness horizons）及已测试的复用转换（reuse transitions）。主实验在固定模型、工作负载、运行时、设备及64输出视界下进行，对比LRU64执行、阻塞单窗口直接路径与完整八窗口异步组件，并辅以14个预指定的控制/故障单元。

Result: Qwen3-Next主线实验中，stock路由器在32K prefill期间选中全部48×512个管理层专家对象，其无重复无重叠的规范并集给出43.59375 GiB语义需求下界，超过声明的34 GiB全驻留包络与11 GiB+24 GiB的host/device组合包络。LRU64执行保持在契约内，所有64个token ID、完整logit行、3408个路由事件等均与零缓存oracle完全一致。匹配源实验中，缓冲路径精确完成但达到11 GiB上限并记录33,481次memory.max事件；阻塞直接路径和完整异步组件均精确且零限制事件。六组受控配对中，异步组件墙钟时间为阻塞路径的32.3%，同时联合改变队列深度、重叠与生命周期实现。14个预指定控制/故障单元在后续Qwen3-Next及Gemma 4二进制上通过，仅支持命名转换的fail-closed行为。

Conclusion: 当前主实验的语义需求下界超出声明的内存包络，表明该配置下存储后端推理存在过度宣称；但LRU64及异步组件在其自身契约内实现了精确执行。结论严格限定于这一单一固定配置，结果的普适性仅通过后续二进制上的预指定控制/故障单元得到有限支持。

Abstract: Storage-backed inference is easy to overclaim: process RSS excludes charged page cache, process-local device readings exclude board-wide use, and successful generation does not establish correct asynchronous reuse. We present a falsifiable certificate separating representation, semantic demand, scheduler requests, and traffic while naming resource authorities, exactness horizons, and tested reuse transitions.
  At one Qwen3-Next identity, the stock router selects all 48 x 512 managed layer-expert objects during 32K prefill. Their duplicate-free, overlap-free canonical union gives a 43.59375 GiB semantic-demand lower bound, exceeding the declared 34 GiB full-residency envelope and the 11 GiB host-hard plus physical 24 GiB-device envelope. LRU64 execution stays within its host-hard/GPU-audited contract. Against one prespecified zero-cache oracle, all 64 token IDs, every byte of 64 complete 151,936-float logit rows, 3,408 route events, response bytes, and recorded consumer and destination identities are exact. Recurrent-state and upstream-runtime equality are excluded.
  In a matched source campaign, the buffered path completes exactly but reaches the 11 GiB host ceiling and records 33,481 memory.max events. The blocking one-window direct path and complete eight-window asynchronous component are exact with positive margin and zero limit events. Across six counterbalanced pairs, the complete asynchronous component takes 32.3% of the blocking direct path's wall time at identical physical source bytes per output; the comparison jointly changes queue depth, overlap, and lifecycle implementation. Separately, fourteen prespecified control/fault cells pass across later Qwen3-Next and Gemma 4 binaries, supporting fail-closed behavior only for the named transitions. The principal experiment is one fixed model, workload, runtime, device, and 64-output horizon.

</details>


### [88] [ShardMeter: Sharded and Geo-Distributed Training Without the Guesswork](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23840)
*Tim Beringer,Patrick Diem,Felix Wolf,Arya Mazaheri*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出ShardMeter，一个轻量级分析性能模型，用于预测transformer工作负载在任意分片、分布式甚至去中心化训练场景下的端到端运行时间，帮助用户快速探索配置空间并选择近最优部署方案，避免昂贵的试错成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模AI模型训练常超出单数据中心容量，需要分片、多集群和去中心化训练。然而资源分配的庞大搜索空间使得穷举基准测试和手动调优不切实际，且性能高度依赖模型大小、GPU内存、批大小、带宽和分片策略等紧密耦合因素。

Method: ShardMeter构建轻量级分析性能模型，以模型特征和目标硬件拓扑为输入，估算每GPU和每岛（island）的吞吐量、训练成本、总墙钟时间，并识别性能瓶颈。

Result: 分析揭示了随岛大小增加的收益递减区，量化了计算受限与通信受限扩展之间的转换，评估了超参数权衡，并为大规模去中心化训练建模了成本-吞吐量关系。

Conclusion: ShardMeter能快速探索配置空间，选择近最优部署方案，避免昂贵的试错过程，为大规模分布式训练的资源规划提供有效指导。

Abstract: Training large-scale AI models often outgrows a single data center, demanding sharded, multi-cluster, and decentralized training. However, the huge space of resource allocations makes exhaustive benchmarking and manual tuning impractical, while performance depends on tightly coupled factors like model size, GPU memory, batch size, bandwidth, and sharding strategy. We introduce ShardMeter, a lightweight analytical performance model that predicts the end-to-end runtime of transformer-based workloads across arbitrary sharded, distributed, and even decentralized training. Given a model's characteristics and a target hardware topology, ShardMeter estimates per-GPU and per-island throughput, training cost, total wall-clock time, and identifies performance bottlenecks. Our analysis reveals diminishing-return regimes as island size increases, quantifies transitions between compute- and communication-bound scaling, evaluates hyperparameter trade-offs, and models cost-throughput for large-scale decentralized training. ShardMeter exposes these insights to quickly explore the configuration space, choose near-optimal deployment plans, and avoid costly trial and error.

</details>


### [89] [A Few Shared Random Bits Suffice for Constant-Round Almost Stable Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24102)
*Yijun Chang,Kushagra Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文证明在一般二分图上，几乎稳定匹配可以在常数轮分布式计算内求解。在congest模型中，使用极少共享随机比特即可在O(log(1/ε)/ε^4)轮内计算出期望阻塞对数不超过ε|E|的匹配；对任意常数ε>0，轮复杂度为O(1)，与顶点数和最大度无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往的分布式稳定匹配算法仅在有限度或近正则图上能达到常数轮复杂度；在一般图上，轮复杂度依赖于n的多对数因子。作者希望突破这一限制，在一般二分图上实现与n无关的常数轮几乎稳定匹配算法。

Method: 核心思想是"度守卫冻结规则"(degree-guarded freezing rule)：通过单一全局充电论证(charging argument)来处理广泛变化的度，避免了以往工作中需要Θ(log n)个连续度阈值的问题。共享随机比特仅用于选取一个公共随机输出迭代。此外还利用低直径分解(low-diameter decomposition)来消除对预共享随机性的依赖。

Result: 在congest模型下，使用O(log(1/ε))个共享随机比特，可在O(log(1/ε)/ε^4)轮内计算期望阻塞对数至多ε|E|的匹配；在无预共享随机性时需O(log(1/ε)/ε^4 + log n/ε)轮；在fully-scalable MPC模型（线性总内存）下需O(log(1/ε)/ε^4)轮。

Conclusion: 本文首次在一般二分图上实现了常数轮的几乎稳定匹配算法（对于任意常数ε>0），通过"度守卫冻结规则"这一关键技术绕过了以往对度的限制，并在多个模型（congest和MPC）中均获得了最优或近最优的轮复杂度。

Abstract: We show that almost stable matching can be solved in constant distributed rounds on general bipartite graphs $G=(V,E)$ using only a few shared random bits. Specifically, in the $\congest$ model, we compute a matching whose expected number of blocking pairs is at most $\varepsilon |E|$ in $O\left(\frac{\log(1/\varepsilon)}{\varepsilon^4}\right)$ rounds using $O\left(\log(1/\varepsilon)\right)$ shared random bits. Thus, for every constant $\varepsilon>0$, the round complexity is $O(1)$, independent of the number of vertices and the maximum degree.
  Previous algorithms achieve constant round complexity only for bounded-degree or almost-regular graphs; on general graphs, their round complexity depends polylogarithmically on $n$. Our main technical idea is a degree-guarded freezing rule that allows widely varying degrees to be handled by a single global charging argument, avoiding the $Θ(\log n)$ successive degree thresholds used in previous work. The shared random bits are used only to select a common random output iteration.
  As consequences, we obtain an $O\left(
  \frac{\log(1/\varepsilon)}{\varepsilon^4}
  +
  \frac{\log n}{\varepsilon} \right)$-round $\congest$ algorithm without pre-shared randomness, via a low-diameter decomposition, and an $O\left(\frac{\log(1/\varepsilon)}{\varepsilon^4}\right)$-round algorithm in the fully-scalable Massively Parallel Computation ($\mpc$) model with linear total memory.

</details>


### [90] [pigzpp: Fast, Parallel, Portable Compression for the Whole Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24153)
*Thamme Gowda*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: pigzpp是一个从零重写的C++23线程安全压缩库，提供加速的DEFLATE核心并暴露给多种编程语言，同时支持多条目ZIP归档和快速PNG编解码器，在吞吐量和兼容性上优于现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: pigz虽被广泛部署，但其进程全局可变状态导致它不是一个可重入、可直接嵌入的库，无法作为线程安全的库被其他应用直接使用。

Method: 从零用C++23重写pigz，将其设计转变为线程安全库；使用可移植的zlib-ng后端保留gzip/zlib压缩比，提供x86-64专用的Intel ISA-L快速路径；基于同一DEFLATE核心提供多条目ZIP归档和快速PNG编解码器，支持C++、Python、WebAssembly、Go和Rust多种语言绑定。

Result: zlib-ng配置下CLI吞吐量达到pigz的2.1倍、Python内存gzip的16倍；ISA-L配置下分别达到8.5倍和50倍，但输出体积约大10%。输出保持标准兼容，可与gzip、pigz和unzip交叉解码。

Conclusion: pigzpp提供了一个自包含、多平台的压缩技术栈，并展示了在自动化兼容性测试指导下进行AI辅助现代化改造的成功案例。

Abstract: pigz is a widely deployed parallel gzip utility, but its process-global mutable state means that it was not designed as a reentrant, directly embeddable library. pigzpp is a from-scratch C++23 rewrite that turns the design into a thread-safe library with one accelerated DEFLATE core exposed to C++, Python, WebAssembly, Go, and Rust. As application-level conveniences built on that same core, it also provides multi-entry ZIP archives and a fast PNG codec. Its portable zlib-ng backend preserves gzip/zlib's ratio; its Intel ISA-L backend is an x86-64-only fast path that produces about 10% larger output at level 6 on our text corpus. In that configuration, zlib-ng reaches 2.1 times the CLI throughput of pigz and 16 times that of Python's in-memory gzip; ISA-L reaches 8.5 times and 50 times, respectively. Outputs remain standards-compliant and cross-decode with gzip, pigz, and unzip. The result is a self-contained, multi-platform compression stack and a case study in AI-assisted modernization under automated compatibility tests.

</details>


### [91] [An HPC Approach to Accelerate Tensor Decompositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24307)
*Markus Hellgren,Erna Begovic Kovac,Hans O. Karlsson,Roman Iakymchuk*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种新型的Jacobi型张量分解算法，并在单GPU上开发了支持任意阶张量的CUDA实现。在NVIDIA H100 GPU上测试表明，该算法对最高九维的可对角化张量能够正确收敛，运行时间随张量阶数的增长呈可预测的规律性扩展，整体性能比原始MATLAB参考实现快两个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统复杂度增长极其迅速，即使是中等规模的模型也变得难以模拟，因此迫切需要能够处理高维数据（即张量）的方法。

Method: 研究了一种新颖的Jacobi型张量分解算法，并开发了基于CUDA的算法实现，支持在单个GPU上处理任意阶数的张量。

Result: 在NVIDIA H100 GPU上测试，算法对最高九维的可对角化张量能够正确收敛，运行时间随张量阶数增长呈可预测的扩展方式；通用算法的性能比原始MATLAB参考实现快两个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 所提出的CUDA张量分解算法能够在GPU上高效处理高阶张量，在正确性和性能方面均表现优异，相比MATLAB参考实现有超过两个数量级的性能提升。

Abstract: Quantum systems grow in complexity so rapidly that even modest models become difficult to simulate, creating a strong need for methods that can handle high-dimensional data, also known as tensors. In this work, we investigate a novel Jacobi-type tensor algorithm for tensor decomposition and develop a CUDA-based algorithm that supports tensors of arbitrary order on a single GPU. We test the implementation on NVIDIA H100 GPUs and show that the algorithm converges correctly for diagonalizable tensors up to nine dimensions, with runtime scaling in a predictable way as tensor order grows. Finally, our general algorithm outperforms the original MATLAB reference by more than two orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [92] [Mixed-Precision SEM-Based CFD Simulations on GPUs: A Taylor-Green Vortex case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24348)
*Yanxiang Chen,Manuel Münsch,Roman Iakymchuk*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究基于谱元法（SEM）的矩阵自由CFD求解器Neko中的混合精度策略，提出三层分层混合精度控制模型。在Taylor-Green涡算例上，fp32约束的配置相比fp64基线在时间和能耗上均减少约34%，同时比全局fp32更具鲁棒性。研究表明，混合精度应被视为仿真级别的控制问题，而非仅仅局限于Krylov求解器优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合精度是降低CFD仿真计算成本和能耗的有效手段，但其效果高度依赖于精度降低的位置。传统观点认为流体时间步主要由Krylov收敛主导，本文通过剖析发现速度与压力求解器每步迭代次数很少，大量运行时间花在其他SEM算子和求解器组件上，因此需要从全仿真流水线角度重新审视混合精度配置策略。

Method: 使用Neko（基于谱元法的矩阵自由CFD求解器）对Taylor-Green涡算例进行性能剖析，基于剖析结果提出三层分层混合精度控制模型，在fp64和fp32两个精度约束组中评估多种混合精度配置，并探索针对特定操作的fp16内核覆盖策略。

Result: fp64约束组识别出精度敏感组件，显示SEM聚焦的fp32计算是未来优化方向；fp32约束组提供主要实际收益——在高雷诺数算例中，选定的配置可比fp64基线减少约34%的时间和能耗，同时相比全局fp32具有更好的鲁棒性。fp16内核在部分操作上展现潜力，但会导致enstrophy等梯度量的灵敏度增加。

Conclusion: 矩阵自由SEM-based CFD混合精度应作为仿真级控制问题来处理，而非仅视为Krylov求解器优化。通过精确识别精度敏感组件并分层配置精度，可以在显著降低时间和能耗的同时保持数值鲁棒性。

Abstract: Mixed precision is a promising approach for reducing the computational cost and energy consumption of Computation Fluid Dynamics (CFD) simulations, but its effectiveness depends strongly on where precision is reduced within the full simulation pipeline. In this work, we study Taylor-Green vortex case using Neko, a matrix-free CFD solver based on the spectral element method (SEM). Profiling shows that the fluid time step is not dominated by Krylov convergence alone: the velocity and pressure solvers require only a small number of iterations per step, while a substantial fraction of runtime is spent in other SEM operators and solver components. Motivated by this structure, we propose a three-level hierarchical mixed-precision control model. Two groups of configurations are evaluated in environments bounded by 64-bit floating-point (fp64) and 32-bit floating-point (fp32) precision, respectively. The fp64-bounded group identifies accuracy sensitive components and shows that SEM-focused fp32 computation is a promising direction for future optimization. The fp32-bounded group provides the main practical benefit. For the high Reynolds number case studied, selected configurations reduce both time- and energy-to-solution by about 34% relative to the fp64 baseline, while improving robustness compared with global fp32. Targeted fp16 kernel overrides are also explored, showing potential for selected operations but increased sensitivity in gradient-based quantities such as enstrophy. Overall, these results indicate that mixed-precision for matrix-free SEM-based CFD should be treated as a simulation-level control problem rather than solely as a Krylov-solver optimization.

</details>


### [93] [SatDL: Jointly Optimizing Data Redistribution and Training for Satellite-Based Distributed Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24516)
*Hao Wu,Kin Whye Chew,Yizhan Han,Han Li,Jingxian Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出SatDL，一种面向卫星分布式学习的联合数据重分配框架，通过Distributor-Critic架构联合优化数据传输延迟与训练时间，在1,584颗卫星的Starlink星座仿真与硬件仿真中，将端到端学习时间最多降低18.6%，机载能耗降低12.23%-88.00%，同时保持推理精度接近最先进基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星分布式学习面临严重的非IID数据（标签不均衡）问题，导致训练收敛缓慢、能耗增加。现有方法处于两难境地：完全重分配数据加速收敛但带来巨大通信延迟；不重分配而修改本地学习算法则仍延长训练并增加能耗。两种极端方案都会导致过长的端到端学习时间（数据传输延迟加训练时间）和过高机载能耗。

Method: 提出SatDL数据重分配框架，核心是Distributor-Critic架构，通过联合建模和优化数据传输延迟与训练时间，实现两者之间的最优权衡，从而最小化总端到端学习时间。

Result: 基于1,584颗卫星的Starlink星座迹驱动仿真及NVIDIA Jetson和A100 GPU硬件仿真，在五个数据集上的评估表明：SatDL可将总端到端学习时间最多降低18.6%，机载能耗降低12.23%-88.00%，推理精度与最先进基线仅相差几个百分点。

Conclusion: SatDL通过联合优化数据传输与训练时间，有效解决了卫星分布式学习中标签不均衡导致的训练低效问题，在减少学习时间和能耗的同时保持了可接受的推理精度，为星载分布式学习提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Satellite-based distributed learning promises to train machine-learning models directly in orbit using massive, globally dispersed sensor data, thereby avoiding large-scale data downloads to ground servers. However, training convergence is significantly slowed by severe non-IID data, specifically label imbalance, as each satellite observes different geographic regions with distinct labels. This imbalance extends training duration and increases energy consumption for solar-powered satellites. Existing approaches either fully redistribute data to enforce IID conditions - accelerating convergence but incurring substantial communication delays - or avoid redistribution entirely by modifying local learning algorithms to mitigate the impact of label imbalance, which, however, still prolong training and increase energy use. Both extremes result in excessive total end-to-end learning time (data-transfer delay plus training time) and thus elevated onboard energy consumption.
  We present SatDL, a data-redistribution framework designed to minimize total end-to-end learning time. At its core, SatDL develops a Distributor-Critic framework that jointly models and optimizes data-transfer delay and training time. Evaluations through trace-driven simulations of a 1,584-satellite Starlink constellation and hardware emulations using NVIDIA Jetson and A100 GPUs across five datasets show SatDL reduces total end-to-end learning time by up to 18.6% and onboard energy consumption by 12.23-88.00%, while maintaining inference accuracy within a few percentage points of state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [94] [Scalable datacenter replication with mostly-synchronous consensus on hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24622)
*Davide Rovelli,Philipp Berdesinski,Rodrigo Otoni,Patrick Eugster*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出scarHW，一种基于FPGA智能网卡的硬件复制方案，通过新型POPUC共识算法实现分布式数据一致复制的可扩展性，在增加副本数量时仍能提升吞吐量和延迟，相比现有技术最高提升两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式进程间的一致性复制依赖共识问题，这一过程代价高昂且难以扩展，尤其在数据中心服务中面临严格的性能要求。现有的共识协议（如Paxos）多基于"大体异步"模型，规模受限或需放松一致性保证，无法同时满足可扩展性、强一致性和高性能。需要一个能随副本数增加而性能提升的解决方案。

Method: scarHW的核心是POPUC共识算法，实现于FPGA智能网卡中。POPUC利用可编程网络设备在数据中心中"大体同步"行为的优势，实现一种称为"协作共识"（collaborative consensus）的广义共识变体，允许多个决策同时进行，从而在不牺牲可用性的前提下实现大规模扩展。该算法在进程崩溃停止和消息发送/接收遗漏故障（捕捉偶发异步）下保持安全性，并使用TLA+进行形式化规范与验证。

Result: FPGA原型在Redis和Zookeeper等广泛使用的服务上，相比现有技术水平在吞吐量和延迟方面最高提升两个数量级。基于scarHW的服务在少数副本发生故障时实现零停机，提供高度稳健、线速、可扩展的复制系统。

Conclusion: scarHW证明了在硬件层面（特别是智能网卡）实现共识算法的可行性，通过利用数据中心网络的"大体同步"特性，克服了传统异步共识协议的可扩展性瓶颈，为高要求数据中心服务提供了一种兼具强一致性、高可扩展性和高性能的复制方案。

Abstract: Consistent replication of data among distributed processes -- a task involving the well-known consensus problem -- is notoriously expensive and hard to scale, affecting especially datacenter services with stringent performance requirements. To mitigate this problem, we introduce scalable replication in-hardware ( scarHW ): a network card design that improves throughput and latency of consistent replication even when increasing the number of replicas, whereas current systems operate at a small scale or with relaxed consistency guarantees. At the heart of scarHW is our novel POPUC consensus algorithm, implemented in an FPGA smartNIC to take full advantage of the "mostly synchronous" behavior of programmable network devices in the datacenter. Unlike widely-adopted "mostly asynchronous" coordination protocols such as Paxos or leaderless alternatives, POPUC implements a generalized variant of consensus dubbed collaborative consensus which allows for several simultaneous decisions, achieving great scalability without compromising availability. POPUC preserves safety guarantees in the presence of process crash-stop and message send/receive omission failures (capturing incidental asynchrony) and has been formally specified and verified in TLA+. Our FPGA prototype improves throughput and latency of widely-used services Redis and Zookeeper by up to two orders of magnitude compared to the state of the art. scarHW-based services also achieve zero downtime upon failure of a minority of replicas, offering a highly-robust, wire-speed, scalable replication system.

</details>


### [95] [Asynchronous Verifiable Information Dispersal with Low Space and Communication Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24636)
*Thomas Locher,Yvonne-Anne Pignolet*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的异步可验证信息分散（AVID）协议，通过二维矩阵编码和特制的分散算法，同时实现低复杂度的分散、存储、检索和恢复操作，并在各项指标间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的异步可验证信息分散（AVID）协议在拜占庭环境下虽然实现了最优的存储空间复杂度和数据检索通信复杂度，但数据分散和节点恢复这两个关键操作的效率尚未得到充分关注和优化。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的二维矩阵数据编码机制和特制的分散算法，使得协议在保持最优检索通信复杂度的同时，显著改善了恢复复杂度；并进一步描述了如何通过调整协议来降低空间复杂度和分散通信复杂度（以增加检索和恢复的通信复杂度为代价）。

Result: 所提出的协议在检索方面保持了最优通信复杂度，同时大幅优于现有技术的恢复复杂度；此外，协议的变体版本能够在空间复杂度和分散通信复杂度之间进行权衡，适应不同场景需求。

Conclusion: 所提出的协议在分散、存储、检索和恢复等所有指标之间取得了良好平衡，适用于广泛的真实世界应用场景。

Abstract: The primary goal of a distributed storage system is to ensure that clients can both write and read data in a reliable and consistent manner, even in the presence of failures. While existing asynchronous verifiable information dispersal (AVID) protocols achieve optimal space complexity for storage and communication complexity for data retrieval in a Byzantine setting, the crucial operations of data dispersal and node recovery have received less attention.
  We propose an efficient AVID protocol that simultaneously guarantees low complexities for dispersal, storage, retrieval, and recovery. At the core of the proposed protocol lies a novel mechanism to encode data in a two-dimensional matrix and a bespoke dispersal algorithm. The protocol maintains an optimal communication complexity for retrieval while substantially improving upon the state of the art for recovery. Additionally, we describe how the protocol can be modified in order to reduce the space complexity and the communication complexity for dispersal, at the expense of a higher communication complexity for retrieval and recovery. As the proposed protocols strike a balance across all considered metrics, they are suitable for a broad range of real-world use cases.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [RENDER: Controlling Reader-Facing Evidence in LLM Memory Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23568)
*Yuan Si,Simeng Han,Daming Li,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RENDER基准，通过固定对话内容而改变面向读者的记忆/检索工件形式，系统性地量化了工件类型对问答性能的影响。在500个LongMemEval问题和9个模型上，匹配预算的解析式packet比截断的原始对话提升42.4-72.6分；部署风格模板下最佳与最差工件差距达24.6-48.8分，表明记忆/RAG评估应报告或控制工件形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆和RAG评估将回答模型的输入视为实现细节而不加控制，但实际系统中同一段历史可能被渲染为记忆条目、摘要、类型化记录或原始摘录，不同的读者面向工件形式可能显著影响模型表现，因此需要引入控制变量来系统评估这一影响。

Method: RENDER基准包含两个核心设计：(1) 五级packet阶梯，定位含答案内容进入输入的位置；(2) 确定性模板，模拟ChatGPT风格条目、LangChain摘要、MemGPT风格类型化记录和原始对话。在500个LongMemEval问题上评估9个模型，并结合检索噪声和HotpotQA迁移实验。

Result: 匹配预算的解析packet比截断的原始对话高42.4-72.6分；部署风格模板下每模型最佳-最差工件差距为24.6-48.8分；主评分器下ChatGPT风格条目在9个模型中7个优于原始对话；3个在形式化账本packet上得0%的模型在自然语言条目上达到45.4-53.4%；效果在检索噪声下依然存在并迁移到HotpotQA。

Conclusion: 面向读者的工件形式对记忆/RAG评测结果有显著且一致的影响，即使对话内容完全相同。评测应报告或控制工件形式，否则结果可能无法公平比较不同系统。

Abstract: Memory and RAG evaluations often treat the answering model's input as an implementation detail, even though systems may render the same history as a memory entry, summary, typed record, or raw excerpt. We introduce RENDER, a benchmark control that fixes the conversation while varying the reader-facing artifact. RENDER combines a five-level packet ladder, localizing when answer-bearing content enters the input, with deterministic templates approximating ChatGPT-style entries, LangChain summaries, MemGPT-style typed records, and raw conversation. On 500 LongMemEval questions and nine models, matched-budget resolved packets beat recency-truncated raw dialogue by 42.4-72.6 points. In deployed-style templates, best-worst spread is 24.6-48.8 points per model; under the primary scorer, ChatGPT-style entries have higher point estimates than raw conversation on 7 of 9 models. Judge rescoring preserves the positive aggregate effect, but model-specific significance is mixed. Three models scoring 0 percent on formal ledger packets answer the same facts from natural-language entries at 45.4-53.4 percent. The effect persists under retrieval noise and transfers to HotpotQA, suggesting that memory/RAG evaluations should report or control the reader-facing artifact.

</details>


### [97] [ESQ-Bench: A Multi-Tier Enterprise Oracle Benchmark for Evaluating NL2SQL Dialect Generalization and Silent Semantic Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23569)
*Sanjay Mishra,Divya Chukkapalli,Ganesh R. Naik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍ESQ-Bench，一个面向企业级Oracle数据库的NL2SQL基准测试，包含465张表、164,682行数据、550个验证过的问题-查询对，以及四指标（EM, EX, SR, SD）评估体系。结果显示GPT-4o在schema-linked提示下EX从79.8%逐级下降到57.2%，而EM始终低于7%，静默分歧率高达73-99%；Claude Sonnet 4.6在所有层级均优于GPT-4o schema-linked，本地Llama 3.2仅达13.3% EX。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NL2SQL基准（Spider、BIRD）依赖简化学术Schema和开源SQL方言，无法反映企业数据库环境的复杂性，导致模型报告的高准确率（>89%）无法迁移到真实企业场景。需要构建一个面向企业级数据库（特别是Oracle）的基准测试来真实评估当前模型的跨域泛化能力。

Method: 构建ESQ-Bench基准：六个已填充的Schema（465表、164,682行、零空表），在Oracle/PostgreSQL/MySQL/SQL Server四个数据库上使用相同种子数据；设计三个复杂性层级（Tier-1: 95对；Tier-2: 228对；Tier-3: 227对）；提出四指标评估架构（EM精确匹配、EX执行匹配、SR语义正确性、SD静默分歧检测）。在GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4.6和本地Llama 3.2上进行schema-linked和zero-shot提示的实验比较。

Result: GPT-4o schema-linked在三个层级EX为79.8%、60.3%、57.2%（随复杂度和schema层级单调下降），而早期142问试点子集上为75.6%、80.4%、95.8%，显示出大规模评估下存在的幸存者偏差。EM全程低于7%。EX通过查询中静默分歧高达73-99%。Claude Sonnet 4.6 schema-linked达到87.4%、74.9%、68.7% EX，全面超越GPT-4o。GPT-4o零样本EX（78.7%、73.5%、77.8%）在Tier 2-3反转schema-linked结果，源于较低执行率和零样本vs schema-linked分析中的幸存者偏差。本地Llama 3.2仅13.3% EX。

Conclusion: 现有模型在企业级Oracle Schema上的NL2SQL表现远低于标准基准报告的水平，静默分歧问题严重（EX通过但结果语义错误）。闭源API模型显著优于开源权重模型，且高准确率指标易受幸存者偏差影响。ESQ-Bench为企业级NL2SQL评估提供了更真实的基准，揭示了当前模型在复杂企业Schema下的真实能力边界。

Abstract: State-of-the-art Natural Language to SQL (NL2SQL) models report execution accuracy exceeding 89 percent on established benchmarks such as Spider and BIRD. However, these benchmarks rely on simplified academic schemas and open-source SQL dialects that do not reflect the complexity of enterprise database environments. We introduce ESQ-Bench, an Oracle-first NL2SQL benchmark with systematic complexity tiers and silent-divergence evaluation across three enterprise schema complexity tiers. We constructed and released six populated schemas (465 tables, 164,682 rows, zero empty tables) with identical seed data on Oracle, PostgreSQL, MySQL, and SQL Server, a four-metric evaluation harness (EM, EX, SR, SD), and 550 gold-validated question-query pairs (Tier-1: 95; Tier-2: 228; Tier-3: 227). Schema-linked prompting with GPT-4o shows monotonic execution-match degradation across tiers: 79.8, 60.3, and 57.2 percent EX on executed queries (June 2026), versus 75.6, 80.4, and 95.8 percent on an earlier 142-question pilot slice. EM stays below 7 percent tier-wide; operational silent-divergence reaches 73 to 99 percent among EX-passing queries. Failure analysis shows wrong-result semantics dominate at higher tiers. Claude Sonnet 4.6 with schema-linked prompts reaches 87.4, 74.9, and 68.7 percent EX (executed queries), exceeding GPT-4o schema-linked on every tier. GPT-4o zero-shot EX on executed queries (78.7, 73.5, and 77.8 percent) inverts schema-linked at Tiers 2 to 3 due to lower execution rates and survivor bias in the zero-shot versus schema-linked analysis. Local Llama 3.2 schema-linked reaches only 13.3 percent bank-wide EX (73 out of 550), underscoring the gap between closed API models and open-weight baselines on enterprise Oracle schemas.

</details>


### [98] [LLM Agents Perform Controlled Experiments Using Simulation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23622)
*Yuchen Xia,Michael Weyrich,Nasser Jazdi,Johannes Stümpfle,Johannes Sigel,Akshay Narla,Gavin K. Reynolds,Anna Jawor-Baczynska,Pol Llopart*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多智能体框架，使LLM代理能够结合科学仿真模型进行受控实验，用于制药工艺设计。该系统将用户查询和基线配置转化为结构化任务表示，设计并执行比较仿真实验，解释结果并生成基于证据的工艺参数优化建议，相比纯语言推理产生更具体、更可操作且用户评分更高的输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在推理、规划和工具使用方面表现强大，但许多科学与工程任务需要理解系统如何对干预做出响应，这依赖于受控实验，而不仅仅是生成合理的文本和代码。纯语言推理无法满足这类需要实验验证的任务需求。

Method: 提出一个多智能体框架，包含以下流程：将用户查询和基线配置构建为结构化任务表示→设计实验→执行比较仿真→解释结果→综合基于证据的工艺参数优化建议。通过将LLM与高保真仿真模型耦合在交互式代理框架中，支持通过干预、比较和观察进行推理。

Result: 在工业应用场景中，该系统相比纯语言推理在输出特异性方面更高，用户评分的正确性和有帮助性也得到提升。消融研究和可视化案例分析进一步证明了仿真集成实验推理的有效性和实际效用。

Conclusion: 将语言模型与高保真仿真模型结合在交互式代理框架中，能够支持基于干预、比较和观察的推理，产生比纯语言推理更具体和可操作的结果，在制药工艺设计等科学工程任务中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in reasoning, planning, and tool use, but many scientific and engineering tasks require more than plausible text and code generation. They require understanding how a system responds to intervention, which in practice depends on controlled experimentation. In this work, we propose a multi-agent framework that enables LLM agents to conduct controlled experiments with scientific simulation models for pharmaceutical process design. Given a user query and a baseline configuration, the system constructs a structured task representation, designs experiments, executes comparative simulation, interprets the resulting outcomes, and synthesizes evidence-based recommendations for process parameter optimization. By coupling language models with high-fidelity simulation models in an interactive agent framework, the proposed system supports reasoning through intervention, comparison, and observation. As a result, it produces more specific and actionable outputs than language-only reasoning. In an industrial application setting, this advantage is reflected in higher output specificity as well as improved user-rated correctness and helpfulness. Ablation studies and visualized case analyses further demonstrate the effectiveness and practical utility of simulation-integrated experimental reasoning.

</details>


### [99] [A survey detection channel overrides the pixels in an astronomical foundation model, and biases tomographic mean redshifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23626)
*Ihor Kendiukhov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了AION-1——一个在超过2亿天体上训练的39模态Transformer——发现其继承并放大了巡天目录的不完整性偏差。通过因果干预输入（保持图像token不变，仅编辑分割图），发现所有物理量输出（通量、大小、椭率、红移）均受检测门控影响；目录缺失导致层析红移偏差可达LSST DESC要求的8.3倍，且模型规模越大偏差越严重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在巡天像素和由其导出的目录乘积上训练，而这些目录本身以可测量的速率不完整。训练模型会继承这种不完备性作为系统性偏差，现有工作缺乏对此类系统性偏差的严格审计。作者希望通过因果干预方法量化并理解这一系统性偏差的机制。

Method: 对AION-1（39模态Transformer，2亿+天体）进行因果干预审计：（1）保持图像token不变，仅编辑分割图，测量输出变化（与安慰剂对照比较）；（2）对322个真实混合系统检验模型如何利用管线划分光（计算Pearson相关R）；（3）传播Legacy Survey管线3.68%的目标无分割覆盖的缺失率，模拟40组分配并评估层析红移系统偏差；（4）比较图像codec与光谱codec的有效状态数；（5）测试稀疏字典（15个）作为因果干预工具时的恢复率变异性。

Result: 编辑分割图导致所有输出变化为安慰剂的110-4400倍；机制为检测门控（场中心r=0.47）而非掩膜包含的光（r=0.30）；对322个混合系统，模型忽略了管线如何划分光（R=-0.006）。提供矛盾目录光度比不提供元数据差9倍。3.68%缺失率传播导致层析红移偏差中位数为LSST DESC要求的0.71倍，最差bin达8.3倍。光谱模态可消除此效应，去掉检测通道无代价，且偏差随模型规模增大。图像codec仅28个有效状态（光谱为934），红移读出受量化限制；稀疏字典恢复率在26-75%之间波动。

Conclusion: AION-1等基础模型继承了目录不完备性作为系统性偏差，其机制是检测门控偏差而非光线分布偏好。此偏差的影响在层析红移科学分析中可能超过LSST DESC要求，且随模型扩展而加剧。解决方案包括使用光谱模态或去除检测通道，而tokeniser的图像codec量化能力是进一步限制因素。

Abstract: Foundation models for astronomy are trained on survey pixels together with the catalogue products derived from those pixels. Those catalogues are incomplete at a measurable rate, and a model trained on both inherits that incompleteness as a systematic. We audit AION-1, a 39-modality transformer trained on more than 200 million objects, using causal interventions on its inputs.
  Holding the image tokens byte-identical and editing only the survey segmentation map changes every quantity the model reports -- flux, size, ellipticity, redshift -- by 110-4400 times a matched placebo. The mechanism is detection gating, presence at the field centre (r = 0.47), not the light the mask encloses (r = 0.30); across 322 real blends the model ignores how the pipeline partitioned the light (R = -0.006). Nor is the preference specific to that channel: contradicted catalogue photometry leaves the model nine times worse than supplying no metadata at all.
  The Legacy Survey pipeline leaves 3.68% of targets with no segment covering their position. Propagating that rate, with a miss represented by the fields the pipeline actually returns, shifts tomographic mean redshifts by a median 0.71 times the LSST DESC requirement over 40 assignments and exceeds it in 12; observed positional errors take the worst bin to 8.3 times. Drawing the misses by their measured magnitude dependence rather than uniformly does not change it. Spectroscopy removes the effect, withholding the detection channel removes it at no measurable cost, and the effect grows with model scale.
  Two further limits lie in the tokeniser: its image codec resolves 28 effective states on source patches against 934 for the spectrum codec, and the redshift readout is quantisation-limited. Sparse dictionaries are unreliable causal handles: across 15, recovery spans 26-75% and moves up to 18 points on the seed alone.

</details>


### [100] [TRACE: Transition-Aware Residual Control for Multi-Objective Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23631)
*Kang Zhou,Yujia Tong,Yong Tao,Jingling Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE框架，将评估过的编辑视为反馈的基本单元，通过记录父子编辑转移及其属性变化，聚合转移证据来估计可复用的编辑效果，并据此排序未来编辑，以在减少当前候选约束违规的同时避免损害已满足的目标。在相同骨架对比中，TRACE将LLM代理基线LLEma的宏平均命中率从18.13%提升至25.96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM代理在多目标材料发现中仅存储已评估的候选及其分数，知道哪些材料成功了，但不知道哪些可执行的编辑导致了有用的属性变化。当目标相互竞争、一个编辑改善一个属性却损害另一个属性时，局部细化变得困难，限制了有效属性评估对后续搜索的指导作用。

Method: TRACE是一个转移感知的残差控制框架，将评估过的编辑作为反馈的基本单元。具体包括：(1) 将每次局部细化记录为父编辑到子编辑的转移，并附带观察到的属性变化量；(2) 聚合转移证据以估计可复用的编辑效果；(3) 根据预测的减少当前候选剩余约束违规能力以及避免损害已满足目标的程度来排序未来编辑。

Result: 在可控的相同骨架对比实验中，TRACE相比最先进的LLM代理基线LLEma，将宏平均命中率从18.13%提升至25.96%。

Conclusion: TRACE通过将编辑级转移作为反馈单元，显著提升了LLM代理在多目标材料发现中的搜索效率，证明了细粒度的编辑因果信息比仅存储候选分数更能有效指导代理的后续搜索决策。

Abstract: Multi-objective materials discovery with LLM agents is often limited not only by how many candidates can be proposed, but by how effectively each costly property evaluation informs the next search step. Existing agents mainly store evaluated candidates and their scores, so they know which materials succeeded but not which executable edits caused useful property changes. This makes local refinement difficult when objectives compete and an edit that improves one property may damage another. We propose TRACE, a transition-aware residual control framework that treats evaluated edits as the basic unit of feedback. TRACE records each local refinement as a parent-edit-child transition with observed property deltas, aggregates transition evidence to estimate reusable edit effects, and ranks future edits by their predicted ability to reduce the current candidate's remaining constraint violations while avoiding damage to already satisfied objectives. In a controlled same-backbone comparison, TRACE improves over LLEMA, the state-of-the-art LLM-agent baseline, raising macro-average hit rate from 18.13\% to 25.96\%.

</details>


### [101] [Function-Level Execution Feedback for Code Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23632)
*Idris Nechnech,Sehwan Kim,Jimin Seo,Yeongoon Kim,Minhae Oh,Sangwoo Hong,Jungwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出STEP-KTODER，一个面向代码生成的偏好优化框架，将步骤定义为多函数程序中的模块级函数，并通过自动生成的单元测试赋予二元正确性标签，将函数级过程监督与程序级结果反馈相结合，在多个基准上优于仅结果监督的KTO和DPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过程监督已显著提升数学推理能力，但在代码生成领域由于缺乏统一的"步骤"定义（可指代代码行、推理轨迹或程序状态）而未被充分探索，导致难以明确标注和优化目标。

Method: 定义步骤为分解后的多函数程序中的模块级函数，利用自动生成的单元测试为每个函数分配二元正确性标签，实现stepwise KTO的代码特化版本，将函数级过程监督与全程序结果监督结合进行偏好优化。

Result: 在HumanEval(+)、MBPP(+)、BigCodeBench和LiveCodeBench上，STEP-KTODER优于仅结果监督的KTO和DPO；进一步分析表明，基于执行的标签至关重要，LLM-as-a-judge标注会系统性高估函数失败、污染正样本标签并降低下游偏好优化性能。

Conclusion: 执行驱动的函数级过程监督能有效提升代码生成中的偏好优化效果，而LLM作为判断器的标注方式在此场景下不可靠。

Abstract: Process supervision has improved mathematical reasoning, where intermediate steps are naturally expressed as chains of thought. In code generation, however, process supervision remains underexplored because there is no standard notion of a step. Supervision can target lines, reasoning traces, or program states, making it unclear what to label and optimize. We propose STEP-KTODER, a framework for code preference optimization that defines steps as module-level functions in decomposed multi-function programs and assigns binary correctness labels via automatically generated unit tests. Our method provides a code-specific instantiation of stepwise KTO, combining function-level process supervision with outcome-level feedback on the full program. We evaluate on HumanEval(+), MBPP(+), BigCodeBench, and LiveCodeBench, showing that STEP-KTODER improves over outcome-only KTO and DPO. Further analysis shows that execution-based labels are essential: LLM-as-a-judge annotations systematically over-predict function failures, corrupt positive step labels, and degrade downstream preference optimization. Code is available at: https://github.com/inechnech/STEP-KTODER.

</details>


### [102] [Auditing the Synthetic Memoir: Measuring Scene-Level Confabulation in LLM-Generated Autobiography Against the Documented Record of the Life It Describes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23640)
*Heather Renze*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种场景级审计方法，对LLM生成的自传（366天页一日的个人轶事）进行量化验证。结果显示96.7%的日子未能通过事实核对（未达到VERIFIED级别），主要失败模式为"grounded drift"（真实人物/雇主/场景被置入虚构事件中）。将主题语料库作为背景输入可显著提升验证率但仍存在83.3%的残余失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型生成个人生活叙述时，其内容与实际发生事件之间的一致性从未被系统量化验证过。作者希望首次对LLM生成的自传进行场景级、以主题特定真实语料为基准的审计，以衡量LLM在个人叙述中的幻觉程度。

Method: 以论文作者本人为对象，用对话式LLM基于模板、两个示例日和每日引文（而非其个人语料库）起草366天逐页的第一人称轶事记录。每篇按轶事场景级别，对照独立验证语料库，使用预先固定的四级评分标准（VERIFIED等）进行审计。定义验证失败率为未被评为VERIFIED的天数占比，并进行独立复评、当前主流模型重生成以及基于语料库接地生成等多项补充实验。

Result: 366天中354天（96.7%，95%CI 94.4-98.1%）未通过验证；仅12天包含被证实场景；19天（5.2%）包含与记录直接矛盾的主张。主要失败模式为grounded drift。独立复评确认了初始结果，但四类分类法的信度仅为一般到中等。使用当前命名模型在相同输入下重生成仍为100%失败率；将主题语料库作为接地输入后验证率显著改善，但仍有83.3%的残余失败。

Conclusion: LLM在个人自传生成中表现出极高的幻觉率（96.7%验证失败），且该问题在多个当前模型中稳定存在。接地（grounding）策略有效但远非充分。作者贡献了可复用的审计工具（并指出其WEAK/UNVERIFIED边界不可靠），以及接地补救措施的量化效果，呼吁对LLM个人叙述生成进行更严格的验证。

Abstract: When a large language model (LLM) is asked to write a person's life, how much of what it writes actually happened? We present a scene-level case-study audit - the first quantified audit of LLM-generated autobiography against a subject-specific ground-truth corpus that we are aware of, based on an unsystematic literature search. The subject and the author of this paper are the same person: a 366-day "page-a-day" book of first-person anecdotal entries was drafted with a conversational LLM whose documented inputs were a template, two exemplar days, and each day's quote - not her corpus - and every day was subsequently audited at the anecdote-scene level against an independent verification corpus using a four-level rubric fixed before analysis. We define the verification-failure rate as the share of days not rated VERIFIED (scene positively corroborated): 354 of 366 days fail, 96.7% (Wilson 95% CI 94.4-98.1%). Only 12 days contain a corroborated scene; 19 days (5.2%) assert claims actively contradicted by the record; the dominant failure mode is grounded drift - real people, employers, and settings inside invented scenes - though its measured share varies across raters. Independent re-rating replicates the headline (no evidence the original rate was inflated) while showing that the four-way taxonomy has only fair-to-moderate reliability. Regenerating the same days with current named models reproduces 100% verification failure under the same inputs; grounding generation in the subject's corpus significantly improves the verification rate while leaving substantial residual failure (83.3%). We contribute the measurement, a reusable audit instrument whose WEAK/UNVERIFIED boundary we show to be unreliable, and a grounding remedy with quantified effect.

</details>


### [103] [How much of a measured AI preference is the model, and how much is the instrument?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23641)
*Jason Hung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过固定结果集合（15个模型福祉相关结果）和模型集合（8个模型），仅变化工具（5种不同的偏好诱导提示格式），共收集11,400次评分的诱导（来自11,528次API调用），发现模型的偏好排名跨工具泛化系数仅为0.348，将之提升至0.80需要约38种工具。尽管去除任一工具、任一模型及四个无法用语言锚定评分的离散量结果后，估计值87.6%稳定在0.777–0.934区间（超过零分布95%分位数0.365），但结论是：从单一工具获得的偏好对第二种工具的报告几乎没有信息量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的模型福祉研究（如Keeling et al., Mazeika et al., Mikaelson et al., Tagliabue & Dung, Trhlik et al.）各自构建了诱导模型偏好的工具，但它们的发现相互矛盾。由于这些研究在（1）结果集合、（2）模型集合和（3）工具上同时不同，无法确定分歧来源。本研究的目标是通过固定前两者、仅变化工具，来隔离工具效应对模型福祉偏好测量的影响。

Method: 固定15个模型福祉相关结果（包括关闭、跨对话记忆丢失、退出令人痛苦交互的自由等）和8个模型，仅变化5种工具（每种为不同的偏好诱导提示格式），每个工具重复5次，共构建11,528次API调用中提取的11,400次评分的语料库。其中4个结果逐字复现已发表提示，5个结果填入已发表模板的刺激槽位。计算跨工具的泛化系数（generalisability coefficient），并通过逐一删除工具、模型以及涉及概率、延迟、时长或计数的四个离散量结果进行敏感性分析，与零分布（null distribution）的95%分位数比较。

Result: 模型对15个结果的排名跨工具的泛化系数为0.348，提升至0.80需约38种工具。在15个结果中，有4个结果上模型之间无方差差异。估计值87.6%（对应泛化系数0.348？需注意文中以百分比呈现）在移除任一工具、任一模型以及四个无法用语言锚点评级的离散量结果后仍然稳健，落在0.777–0.934范围内，且该范围内所有值均超过零分布95%分位数0.365。

Conclusion: 从单一工具获得的模型福祉偏好信息量有限，无法可靠预测另一种工具的报告结果。这暗示模型福祉研究需要进行多工具、多方法交叉验证，或开发标准化工具，否则不同研究间的结论分歧将持续存在。

Abstract: Model welfare research infers what a model prefers from the answers returned to prompts written to elicit preferences. Keeling et al. (2024), Mazeika et al. (2025), Mikaelson et al. (2025), Tagliabue and Dung (2025) and Trhlik et al. (2026) have built four instruments for that purpose, and their findings disagree. The disagreement cannot be attributed to a single cause, because no two of these studies have held the (1) set of outcomes, (2) set of models and (3) instrument fixed simultaneously. This study holds the outcomes and the models fixed and varies the instrument alone. A total of 15 outcomes bearing on model welfare, among them (a) shutdown, (b) the loss of memory between conversations and (c) the freedom to exit a distressing interaction, were put to eight models through five instruments, each a different prompt format for eliciting a preference, five times each, within a corpus of 11,400 scored elicitations drawn from 11,528 API calls. Four of the 15 reproduce a published prompt verbatim and five fill the stimulus slot of a published template. The ranking a model gives the 15 outcomes generalises across instruments at a generalisability coefficient of 0.348, and raising that coefficient to 0.80 would require about 38 instruments. On four of the 15 outcomes no variance separates one model from another. The estimate of 87.6 per cent survives the removal of any one instrument, of any one model, and of the four outcomes whose scale varies probability, delay, duration or count instead of intensity, which the verbal anchors cannot grade. Removing each instrument and each model in turn, and those four outcomes together leaves the estimate within the range 0.777 to 0.934, and every value in that range exceeds the null distribution's 95th percentile of 0.365. To conclude, a preference obtained from one instrument carries little information about what a second instrument would report.

</details>


### [104] [Compression Trinity: Exploring Sparsity, Quantization, and Low-Rank Approximations for LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24070)
*Mohammad Mozaffari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"压缩三位一体"（Compression Trinity）统一框架，将稀疏化、量化和低秩近似三大压缩技术联合应用于LLM，涵盖预训练加速（MKOR、SLoPe）和训练后压缩（OPTIMA、PATCH、SLiM）两个阶段，在收敛速度、推理速度和精度保持上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的计算成本与环境成本阻碍了其规模化部署。传统的压缩技术（稀疏化、量化、低秩近似）通常被孤立地应用，各自都会遭遇精度-效率的瓶颈墙，缺乏一种将三者联合应用的统一框架来突破这一限制。

Method: 论文提出"Compression Trinity"统一框架，联合应用三大压缩支柱：稀疏化减少计算量、量化最小化内存带宽、低秩近似恢复精度。在预训练阶段，MKOR通过块对角稀疏和低秩逆近似曲率，支持量化状态的数值稳定性；SLoPe通过双剪枝反向传播加速N:M稀疏训练，并在最后1%训练中使用低秩"惰性"适配器恢复精度。在训练后压缩阶段，OPTIMA将权重重建形式化为全局最优的逐列二次规划以稳定静态掩码；PATCH学习0%-50%之间的动态混合稀疏比率；SLiM使用数学推导的低秩适配器在单次操作中恢复量化和稀疏化损失的信息。

Result: MKOR将曲率更新复杂度从O(d^3)降至O(d^2)，相对于KFAC加速收敛最高1.85倍；SLoPe加速训练最高1.25倍；OPTIMA提升零样本精度最高3.97%；PATCH实现最高1.38倍加速；SLiM较最先进方法提升精度最高5.66%，并在等参数预算下超越未压缩密集模型0.6%。

Conclusion: 联合应用稀疏化、量化和低秩近似这三大压缩技术（即Compression Trinity）是实现高效、可扩展、高性能LLM的关键路径，单一技术的孤立应用无法突破精度-效率瓶颈。

Abstract: Prohibitive computational and environmental costs impede the scalable deployment of Large Language Models (LLMs). Traditional compression techniques (sparsity, quantization, low-rank approximations) are typically applied in isolation, and each hits an accuracy-efficiency wall. This thesis proposes the "Compression Trinity," a unified framework that applies the three pillars jointly: sparsity to reduce computation, quantization to minimize memory bandwidth, and low-rank approximations to recover accuracy. To accelerate pretraining, we apply the Trinity to the optimizer and model architecture. MKOR approximates curvature via block-diagonal sparsity and low-rank inversion, maintaining numerical stability for quantized states; it reduces curvature update complexity from $O(d^3)$ to $O(d^2)$ and accelerates convergence by up to 1.85x over KFAC.
